Conclusion: ASR置信度是有效的无标签语音质量代理指标，能以更少数据获得更好SSL预训练效果；系统架构对比揭示了不同架构在匹配参数量下的性能差异，为语音SSL数据筛选与架构选择提供实证指导。

Abstract: Broadcast archives offer scale and diversity for self-supervised learning (SSL) but contain substantial noisy, non-speech, and multilingual content. Prior work typically relies on voice activity detection or language identification (LID) to extract speech, ensuring presence rather than quality. Whether and how to filter unlabeled speech by quality for SSL, particularly from noisy broadcasts, remains underexplored. We present a large-scale Finnish SSL study starting from 2.1M hours of unconstrained broadcast audio. We propose automatic speech recognition (ASR) confidence as a proxy for pretraining data quality and show 3-7% relative WER improvements over LID-based filtering despite a smaller training set. We further address the lack of controlled architectural comparisons in speech SSL by evaluating Transformer, Macaron-Net, Conformer, and E-Branchformer at matched parameter budgets. Our models improve upon prior Finnish SSL baselines, particularly at Base scale and out-of-domain.

</details>


### [36] [BenCoDiC: A Large-Scale Contextual Cyberbullying Corpus for Bengali Political News Discourse FULL VERSION](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13121)
*Md. Nesarul Hoque,Rudra Pratap Deb Nath,Umme Salma,Shima Chakraborty,Md. Hanif Seddiqui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个大规模、多维度的孟加拉语网络霸凌语料库BenCoDiC，包含50,250条来自YouTube政治新闻视频及其评论区的文本实例，保留了标题-评论-回复的对话结构，并通过九维星型模式组织元数据。采用两层人工标注并经专家验证，达到了Fleiss' κ=0.86和Krippendorff's α=0.86的高一致性。实验表明，引入对话上下文能在Transformer模型上带来稳定但适度的性能提升。语料库已公开，支持上下文感知的霸凌检测及施害者行为与网络分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有孟加拉语网络霸凌语料库在上下文信息、标注透明度、规模、可获取性及多维分析支持等方面存在不足，限制了鲁棒检测模型的开发以及对施害者行为和网络的全面研究。

Method: 1) 从100个YouTube政治新闻视频及其讨论区收集50,250条文本实例，保留标题-评论-回复层级关系；2) 设计九维星型模式（用户、视频、文本内容、上下文、时间、互动、政党、目标身份、语言类别）组织元数据；3) 初步评估五个AI聊天机器人标注未达标准，转为两层人工标注流程并经专家验证，计算Fleiss' κ和Krippendorff's α评估一致性；4) 开展统计分析刻画语料库特征；5) 使用Transformer模型对比隔离文本分类与融合对话上下文的检测效果。

Result: 人工标注达到Fleiss' κ=0.86和Krippendorff's α=0.86的高一致性。统计分析揭示了语料库在各维度的分布特征。Transformer实验显示，引入对话上下文相比仅使用孤立文本，在网络霸凌检测任务上获得适度但持续的性能提升。

Conclusion: BenCoDiC填补了孟加拉语网络霸凌研究中大规模、上下文丰富、多维标注语料库的空白，为上下文感知的霸凌检测、施害者行为建模及网络分析提供了高质量资源。语料库已公开发布，促进后续研究。

Abstract: Cyberbullying is a growing concern in online communities, yet existing Bengali corpora remain limited in contextual information, annotation transparency, scale, accessibility, and support for multidimensional analysis, thereby hindering robust detection and comprehensive study of harasser behaviors and networks. To address these gaps, we introduce the Bengali Contextual and Diverse Cyberbullying (\textit{BenCoDiC}) corpus, a publicly available contextual cyberbullying resource for Bengali political news discourse comprising 50,250 textual instances collected from 100 YouTube videos and their associated discussions. The corpus preserves caption--comment--reply relationships and is organized through a nine-dimensional star schema covering users, videos, textual content, context, time, engagement, political parties, target identities, and language categories. Five AI chatbots were initially evaluated for annotation but did not meet the predefined reliability criterion. Consequently, a two-tier human annotation process with expert verification was adopted, achieving Fleiss' $κ=0.86$ and Krippendorff's $α=0.86$. Statistical analyses characterize the corpus, while transformer-based experiments show that incorporating conversational context yields modest but consistent improvements over isolated-text classification. \textit{BenCoDiC} supports context-aware cyberbullying detection and perpetrator-oriented behavioral and network analysis. The corpus is available at https://github.com/bencodic/BenCoDiC-Corpus.

</details>


### [37] [Neurograf v1: An End-to-End Romanian-First Language Model Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13134)
*Ionut Adrian Rotaru*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Neurograf v1，一个面向罗马尼亚语的端到端语言模型训练管道，包含大规模语料库、分词器、多规模解码器模型、指令微调助手和嵌入模型，验证了低资源欧洲语言从头训练语言模型的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为低资源欧洲语言（罗马尼亚语）从头训练语言模型的可行性，填补该语言在大规模语言模型研究中的空白。

Method: 构建635 GB罗马尼亚语语料库RoSfera（1.757亿文档），训练80k字节级BPE分词器，训练五个规模从160M到6B参数的解码器模型（共1310亿tokens），进一步进行指令微调得到三个助手模型，对比训练两个嵌入模型，在ELI-NP/IFIN-HH计算设施上完成训练与评估。

Result: 模型族展现出验证损失的持续缩放规律，罗马尼亚语事实与文化知识随模型规模增长。在WWTBM基准上，Neurograf-6B在罗马尼亚语语境问题上达69.89%准确率，Neurograf-3.2B达67.38%。计算最优分析得出参数缩放指数约0.423。同时识别出变音符号保留、语料质量、数据组成、分词效率等实际挑战。

Conclusion: Neurograf v1作为实验性演示系统，证明了在统一训练管道中开发罗马尼亚语优先的基础模型、指令跟随助手和语义嵌入模型的可行性，为低资源语言模型研究提供了参考。

Abstract: We present Neurograf v1, an experimental end-to-end Romanian-first language-model pipeline developed to investigate the feasibility of training language models from scratch for a lower-resource European language. The pipeline comprises RoSfera, a 635 GB Romanian corpus containing 175.7 million documents; an 80k byte-level BPE tokenizer; five decoder-only models ranging from 160M to 6B parameters, trained on 131 billion tokens; three instruction-tuned assistants; and two contrastively trained embedding models. Training and evaluation were conducted using computing infrastructure at ELI-NP/IFIN-HH.
  The model family exhibits consistent scaling of validation loss and increasing Romanian factual and cultural knowledge with model size. On the 1,000-question WWTBM benchmark, Neurograf-6B achieves 69.89% accuracy on Romanian-context questions, while Neurograf-3.2B reaches 67.38%. Compute-optimal analysis yields a parameter-scaling exponent of approximately 0.423.
  Neurograf v1 serves as an experimental demonstrator rather than a production system. The study identifies practical challenges involving diacritic preservation, corpus quality, data composition, and tokenization efficiency, while demonstrating the feasibility of developing Romanian-first base models, instruction-following assistants, and semantic embedding models within a unified training pipeline.

</details>


### [38] [SOAR: Source-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Precise Credit Assignment for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13157)
*Shengze Huang,Zihao Tang,Jinluan Yang,Xin Yu,Zhenghao Lin,Xiao Liu,Zengxuan Wen,Yeyun Gong,Haizhen Huang,Weiwei Deng,Feng Sun,Qi Zhang,Fei Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出SOAR（Source-Aware Reinforcement Learning）框架，通过将外部证据和内部知识显式解耦为并行可观测流，并引入源一致性奖励机制，有效解决了搜索智能体中的证据捏造问题，在3B和7B模型上分别提升7.3%和5.4%性能，将证据捏造减少74.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习搜索智能体常出现证据捏造现象，即智能体绕过外部信息(E_ext)而依赖不可靠的内部知识(E_int)作为捷径，这源于推理轨迹的固有不透明性导致无法准确归因信息来源。

Method: SOAR包含两大核心组件：(1) Source-Aware Workflow：将单体推理转化为源显式轨迹，将E_ext和E_int解耦为并行、可观测的流；(2) Source-Consistent Reward：引入源一致性奖励指导策略演化，通过显式的信息源归因实现不同知识流之间的精确信用分配。

Result: 在知识密集型基准测试中，SOAR在3B和7B骨干模型上分别比基线提升7.3%和5.4%的性能，同时将证据捏造率降低74.8%，验证了方法在提升性能和缓解捏造方面的有效性。

Conclusion: SOAR通过显式的信息源归因机制，显著改善了知识密集型任务上的推理表现，有效缓解了证据捏造问题，促进了更忠实、更有依据且鲁棒性更强的推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for search agents often suffers from evidence fabrication, a failure mode where agents bypass external information ($E_{ext}$) and fabricate an incorrect answer. We argue that this fabrication arises from an over-reliance on unreliable internal knowledge ($E_{int}$) as shortcuts due to the inherent opacity of reasoning trajectories. To address this, we propose SOAR (SOurce-Aware Reinforcement learning), a paradigm that transforms monolithic reasoning into source-explicit trajectories. SOAR (i) disentangles $E_{ext}$ and $E_{int}$ into parallel, observable streams via a Source-Aware Workflow, and (ii) incorporates Source-Consistent Reward to guide the policy evolution. By making information-source attribution explicit, SOAR enables precise credit assignment between different knowledge streams. Evaluations show that SOAR outperforms baselines on 3B and 7B backbones by 7.3% and 5.4%, while reducing evidence fabrication by 74.8%. These confirm that SOAR significantly improves performance on knowledge-intensive benchmarks while effectively mitigating evidence fabrication, fostering more faithful and grounded reasoning with enhanced robustness.

</details>


### [39] [Scaling Agentic Data for Long-Horizon Terminal Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13166)
*Shun Zou,Ziyu Ma,Yi Zou,Yong Wang,Lin Chen,Zehui Chen,Guanhua Chen,Xiangxiang Chu,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TerminalHorizon 是一个自动化数据合成引擎，用于生成长时序终端智能体轨迹。它从真实专业工作出发，通过独立执行识别被省略的关键工作、利用证据指导环境重构、并在同一目标下渐进式扩展任务，构建了 1.5K 任务环境并微调 Qwen3.5-27B 得到 TerminalHorizon-27B。该模型在 12 个基准上均获显著性能提升，RSI 案例研究（每个 12 小时预算）展示其能持续实验、基于反馈改进并保留有效改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有短轨迹无法完整捕捉智能体在长时程任务中的核心行为模式：通过探索积累证据、依据证据做判断、根据反馈持续调整策略、将中间结果转化为最终交付物。环境合成往往预先完成或简化关键工作，剥夺了智能体探索、实现、验证的机会，导致数据缺乏真实的智能体行为链条。

Method: 提出 TerminalHorizon 全自动数据合成引擎：1) 以真实专业工作为起点；2) 通过独立执行识别构建过程中被省略的关键工作；3) 利用执行产生的证据指导环境重构，还原探索与验证过程；4) 在同一目标下渐进式扩展任务，引入基于先前结果的新需求，强制智能体进行进一步探索和改进。基于此构建 1.5K 任务环境，对 Qwen3.5-27B 进行监督微调得到 TerminalHorizon-27B。

Result: 在涵盖编码智能体任务、真实生产环境智能体任务、通用推理的 12 个基准上均实现显著性能提升。多个 RSI（递归自我改进）案例研究（每个分配 12 小时研究预算）表明，模型能在初始解决方案基础上继续实验、根据反馈改进方法，并在最终交付物中保留有效改进，展现出持续自主工作的能力。

Conclusion: TerminalHorizon 通过自动化合成长时序轨迹，成功捕捉了智能体在真实专业任务中探索、证据积累、策略调整和交付物生成等核心行为模式，显著提升了终端智能体在长时程复杂任务上的表现，并验证了其持续自我改进的潜力，为构建真正自主的长时程智能体提供了关键数据基础。

Abstract: Terminal agents are moving beyond local operations toward completing real-world professional tasks. Short trajectories can demonstrate commands and tool calls, but they do not fully show how agents accumulate evidence through exploration, use it to inform judgments, continually adjust strategies based on feedback, and turn intermediate results into final deliverables. High-quality long-horizon trajectories capture these agentic behavioral patterns and their connections within a single problem-solving process, providing richer experience for sustained autonomous work. However, environment synthesis may complete or simplify the key work underlying these patterns in advance and thus remove the need for exploration, implementation, and verification that the agent was meant to perform. To address this, we introduce TerminalHorizon, a fully automated data synthesis engine for long-horizon terminal agents. Starting from real-world professional work, it uses independent execution to identify key work omitted during construction and uses the resulting evidence to guide environment reconstruction. To extend these patterns across stages, it progressively expands tasks under the same goal, introducing new requirements that build on prior results and call for further exploration and improvement. Using this engine, we construct 1.5K task environments and perform supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.5-27B on the resulting dataset to obtain TerminalHorizon-27B. We achieve substantial performance gains on all 12 benchmarks spanning coding-agent tasks, agentic tasks in real-world production settings, and general reasoning. Multiple RSI case studies, each with a 12-hour research budget, further demonstrate the model's ability to continue experimenting beyond an initial solution, improve its methods based on feedback, and retain effective improvements in its final deliverables.

</details>


### [40] [PreSTO: Predictive Subtree Prefetching for Fast LLM Power Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13171)
*Nan Jiang,Panagiotis Theodoropoulos,Wei Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PreSTO通过预测子树预取加速PowerMH采样，在保持Metropolis-Hastings转移核不变的前提下，批量生成提议并按原接受规则遍历，实现1.7-2.3倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幂采样虽能提升LLM测试时推理能力，但其采样器PowerMH因Metropolis-Hastings更新的顺序性而缓慢：每个接受/拒绝决定决定下一提议的生成状态。现有工作通过改变采样器或模型降低成本，但本文旨在不改变MH转移核的情况下加速。

Method: PreSTO将未来MH结果建模为接受/拒绝决策树：每次模型调用批量生成一个“预取子树”的提议，再按原MH接受规则跟随实际路径。观察到MH仅到达每个预取子树稀疏、近乎一元的部分，进一步设计“预测遍历规则”选择子树以最大化预测完成的转移数。

Result: 跨七个数据集和五个基础LLM，PreSTO在PowerMH、EntropyCut、MultiTryMH上分别达到中位数1.71×、1.91×、2.33×加速；前两者模型调用减少中位数3.9×，每次调用仅慢2.1×。在LCB V6上Qwen3.5-9B，预测遍历规则比广度优先遍历再快1.25×-1.37×。

Conclusion: PreSTO通过预取子树与预测遍历，在不改变MH核的情况下显著减少模型调用并加速幂采样，具有广泛的适用性和理论保证。

Abstract: Power sampling improves large language model (LLM) reasoning at test time by sampling from a sharpened version of the base model's distribution. Its sampler, PowerMH, is slow because its Metropolis--Hastings (MH) updates are sequential: each accept/reject decision sets the state from which the next proposal is generated. Prior work reduces this cost by changing the sampler or the model (e.g., sequential Monte Carlo, approximate lookahead, distillation). We propose Predictive SubTree Prefetching (PreSTO), which accelerates PowerMH by prefetching future proposals and provably leaves its MH transition kernel unchanged. PreSTO views future MH outcomes as a tree of accept/reject decisions: in each model call, it generates the proposals of a \textit{prefetched subtree} of this tree in one batch, then follows the realized path with the original MH acceptance rule. We also observe that MH reaches only a sparse, nearly unary part of each prefetched subtree, we further design a \textit{predictive traversal rule} that chooses the subtree to maximize the predicted number of completed transitions. Across seven datasets and five base LLMs, PreSTO achieves median speedups of $1.71\times$, $1.91\times$, and $2.33\times$ on PowerMH and its follow-ups EntropyCut and MultiTryMH, respectively; on the first two, it makes a median of $3.9\times$ fewer model calls, each only $2.1\times$ longer. On LCB V6 with Qwen3.5-9B, the predictive traversal rule is a further $1.25\times$--$1.37\times$ faster than the default breadth-first traversal.

</details>


### [41] [SharP: Soft and Hard Prompt-Guided Augmentation with LLM for Low Resource Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13207)
*Ziyu Luo,Wei Li,Haoping Huang,Kecheng Liu,Min Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SharP 框架，利用软硬双提示引导大语言模型生成任务特定的合成样本，结合边界感知策略和双目标优化，解决低资源假新闻检测中标注数据稀缺及现有增强方法生成内容过于泛化的问题，提升检测模型的鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源假新闻检测面临标注数据稀缺、事实核查成本高、新兴主题和领域基准覆盖不足等挑战。现有数据增强方法（同义词替换、GAN、LLM 生成）虽能生成流畅文本，但往往过于泛化，缺乏任务特定相关性，难以捕捉真假新闻的细微欺骗模式。

Method: 提出 SharP 边界感知文本生成框架：1) 双提示机制——硬提示提供结构化指导与事实约束，软提示引入数据学习到的语义模式，二者结合引导 LLM 生成贴近新闻特征的任务特定样本；2) 边界感知策略——引导生成向分类器低置信度区域靠拢，澄清真假新闻的细微决策边界；3) 双目标优化——平衡与源数据的语义对齐及分类器反馈，生成既领域一致又有利于细化决策边界的样本。

Result: SharP 生成的合成样本在保持领域一致性的同时，有效细化了分类决策边界，在低资源假新闻检测任务上显著提升了模型的鲁棒性与泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过软硬双提示引导的边界感知生成框架，结合双目标优化，可利用 LLM 生成高质量、任务特定的合成数据，有效缓解低资源场景下的数据稀缺问题，推动假新闻检测模型在新兴主题和领域上的实用化部署。

Abstract: Fake news detection under low resource conditions is challenged by the scarcity of labeled data and the difficulty of capturing subtle deceptive patterns. In this work, we focus on low resource fake news detection, where only a small labeled set is available for training, while evaluation is performed under a fixed and unchanged test protocol. This setting is motivated by costly, ambiguous fact-checking and emerging topics and domains that are poorly covered by existing benchmarks. Existing data augmentation methods, such as synonym substitution, generative adversarial networks, and large language model (LLM)-based generation, often produce fluent but overly generic content, lacking the task-specific relevance needed for accurate detection. To address this, we propose SharP, a boundary-aware text generation framework guided by soft and hard prompts, which leverages LLMs as tools adapted to produce task-specific synthetic samples. SharP employs a dual prompt mechanism: hard prompts provide structured guidance and factual constraints, while soft prompts introduce learned semantic patterns from the data. These two types of prompts are combined to guide the generation process, enabling the model to produce samples that closely align with the characteristics of news. To further enhance detection performance, we incorporate a boundary-aware strategy that steers generation toward areas where the classifier is less confident, helping to clarify subtle distinctions between real and fake content. In addition, we adopt a dual objective optimization that balances semantic alignment with the source data and classifier feedback. This encourages the generation of samples that are both domain-consistent and helpful for refining decision boundaries, ultimately improving model robustness and generalization in low resource fake news detection.

</details>


### [42] [How Reliable are AMR Parser Outputs? Uncertainty Estimation for AMR Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13236)
*Anuj Kumar,Shintaro Ozaki,Yamuna Prasad,Katsuhiko Hayashi,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次在黑盒设置下研究AMR解析器的不确定性估计，利用top-k束搜索候选通过成对相似度计算不确定性，并用预测拒绝比评估，揭示了解析性能与不确定性的关系，验证了光束候选分布的可靠性，分析了评价指标对不确定性评估的影响，并识别出性能与可靠性平衡最好的解析器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AMR解析器性能显著提升，但何时可信任其输出仍 largely unexplored（很大程度上未被探索），缺乏不确定性估计机制来判断解析结果的可靠性。

Method: 在黑盒设置下，利用top-k束搜索候选，通过计算候选间的成对相似度来估计不确定性，采用预测拒绝比（Prediction Rejection Ratio）进行评估；同时表征解析性能与不确定性的关系、评估光束候选分布的可靠性、分析评价指标对不确定性评估的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明不确定性估计作为评估AMR解析器的互补维度具有价值，成功识别出在性能与可靠性之间达到最佳平衡的解析器。

Conclusion: 不确定性估计为AMR解析器评估提供了重要的互补视角，有助于在实际应用中判断何时可以信任解析输出，推动了AMR解析可靠性研究的发展。

Abstract: Recent advances have significantly improved the performance of Abstract Meaning Representation (AMR) parsers, yet determining when their outputs can be trusted remains largely unexplored. This work presents the first study of uncertainty estimation for AMR parsers in a black-box setting using top-k beam-search candidates. Uncertainty is estimated from pairwise candidate similarities and evaluated using the Prediction Rejection Ratio. This work characterises the relationship between parsing performance and uncertainty, evaluates the reliability of beam candidate distributions, analyses the effect of evaluation metrics on uncertainty assessment, and identifies parsers that provide the strongest balance between performance and reliability. Experimental results show the value of uncertainty estimation as a complementary dimension for evaluating AMR parsers.

</details>


### [43] [Do Prompt Optimizers Break Requirements They Are Not Scored On? A Preregistered Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13287)
*Sangdo Han,Moonsoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究提示词优化器（GEPA、MIPROv2、TextGrad）在提升目标指标（如 Exact Match）时，对未评分需求（引用有效性、输出格式等）的副作用。预注册实验显示三种优化器均导致显著回归，尤其在 HotpotQA 上引用有效性净回归 10.7–41.6 分，而 Exact Match 上升 21–34 分。事后分析发现主要原因是 200-token 输出上限导致 13.9%–47.0% 响应在答案出现前被截断；放宽至 1024-token 后提取失败消失，内容回归 <1 分，Exact Match 进一步提升。结论：副作用比假设窄，优化后的提示词更准确但破坏显式输出契约，宽松目标指标未暴露成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示词优化器仅针对单一目标指标优化，但实际提示词常包含未被指标评分的硬性需求（格式、引用、拒答等）。作者旨在量化优化器对这些未评分需求的副作用，验证“优化提升目标指标会破坏其他需求”的假设。

Method: 预注册研究，在 HotpotQA、SQuAD 2.0、IFEval 三个基准上运行 GEPA、MIPROv2、TextGrad 三种优化器，使用确定性检查统计每项输出对未评分需求的违规数。设置 200-token 输出上限，对比优化前后的 Exact Match 与引用有效性等指标。事后进一步分析输出截断问题，并实验 1024-token 上限。

Result: 预注册标准下三优化器均产生回归：HotpotQA 引用有效性净回归 10.7–41.6 分，Exact Match 涨 21–34 分。事后分解显示优化后提示词诱导先推理后输出 JSON，在 200-token 上限下 13.9%–47.0% 响应在答案前截断；可解析输出中净回归 ≤3 分。1024-token 上限下提取失败消失，内容回归 <1 分，Exact Match 继续上升。两个简单门控未通过预注册标准。

Conclusion: 副作用比假设窄：优化后的提示词在内容准确性上更优，但会打破显式输出契约（如格式、引用位置）；宽松的目标指标（Exact Match）未能反映这种契约破坏的成本。实际部署需关注输出长度限制与格式合规性的权衡。

Abstract: Prompt optimizers rewrite a prompt to raise one target metric, but prompts also state requirements the metric does not score, such as an output format, citations, or abstention. In a preregistered study, we run GEPA, MIPROv2, and TextGrad on HotpotQA, SQuAD 2.0, and IFEval and count per-item violations of unscored requirements with deterministic checks. By the preregistered criterion, all three optimizers produce regressions, substantially on HotpotQA, where net regression in citation validity reaches 10.7 to 41.6 points while exact match rises by 21 to 34 points. A post-hoc decomposition changes this reading. The optimized prompts elicit reasoning before the required JSON object, and under the 200-token output cap used throughout, 13.9% to 47.0% of responses end before any answer appears. Among parseable outputs, net regression is within 3 points of zero. With a 1,024-token cap, extraction failures disappear, content regression stays below 1 point, and exact match rises further. Two simple gates fail the preregistered criterion. The side effect is narrower than hypothesized: optimized prompts are more accurate and break an explicit output contract, and a lenient target metric does not show the cost.

</details>


### [44] [Agreement Without Alignment: Label-Free Rubric Clarification Increases LLM-Judge Consensus but Not Agreement with Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13303)
*Sangdo Han,Moonsoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无标签程序，利用LLM评委面板间的分歧自动修复评分标准，实验显示该方法能提升评委间一致性，但未改善与人类专家评分的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评委面板越来越常见，面板成员间的分歧常被视为评分标准不清晰的症状。作者旨在测试一种无需人工标签的程序，将这种分歧转化为评分标准的自动修复，从而提高评委间的一致性。

Method: 将评分标准分解为子准则，在开发集上按跨评委分歧排序，仅让编辑模型重写分歧最大的子准则；另设阶段尝试将可文本检查的子准则移交给确定性检查器（评估中未生效）。全程不使用人工标签选择或编辑条款，仅用于最终评估。预注册四个假设，采用五个来自三家提供商的评委，在SummEval、HealthBench和USR Topical-Chat三个数据集上评估六种评分标准条件，共129,770次评委请求。

Result: 在SummEval上，该程序使跨评委Krippendorff's α较原始评分标准提升0.069（95% CI 0.046–0.092），较随机编辑同等数量非争议条款的对照组提升0.074。但与专家评分的一致性（Kendall τ）无显著变化（差异-0.007，95% CI -0.037–0.021）。在HealthBench和USR上，跨评委一致性与人工一致性均未达预注册效应标准。预注册的次要比较显示，编辑争议条款后的人工一致性在点估计上均低于编辑对照条款，但置信区间均包含零。

Conclusion: 基于分歧选择条款进行编辑能显著提高评委间一致性，优于随机编辑任意条款；然而在实验设定下，这种额外共识并未使评委判断更接近人类标注者。作者将发布代码、冻结评分标准、项目标识符和评委分数。

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly deployed as panels, and disagreement among panel members is often read as a symptom of an unclear rubric. We test a label-free procedure that turns this disagreement into rubric repairs. The procedure decomposes each rubric into sub-criteria, ranks them by cross-judge disagreement on a development split, and has an editor model rewrite only the most contested ones. An additional stage that moves text-checkable sub-criteria to deterministic checkers transferred none in our evaluation. Human labels are never used to select or edit clauses; they are used only to evaluate the outcome. We pre-specified four hypotheses in a version-controlled protocol and evaluated six rubric conditions with a panel of five judges from three providers on SummEval, the HealthBench meta-evaluation, and USR Topical-Chat, for a total of 129,770 judge requests, excluding retries. On SummEval, the procedure raised inter-judge Krippendorff's $α$ by 0.069 (95% CI 0.046 to 0.092) over the original rubric and by 0.074 over a control that applied the same edits to an equal number of randomly chosen clauses that were not selected. Agreement with expert ratings showed no detectable change (Kendall $τ$ difference -0.007, 95% CI -0.037 to 0.021). On HealthBench and USR, neither inter-judge agreement nor human agreement met the preregistered effect criteria. In a preregistered secondary comparison, human agreement after editing the contested clauses was lower than after editing the control clauses on all three datasets in point estimate, although no interval excluded zero. Selecting clauses by disagreement makes judges agree with each other more than editing arbitrary clauses does, but in our setting the added consensus did not bring the judges closer to human annotators. We will release code, frozen rubrics, item identifiers, and judge scores.

</details>


### [45] [Host-Aware Web Crawling for LLM Pretraining Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13321)
*Malte Ostendorff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究网络爬虫如何在主机间和主机内分配预算以收集更多高质量LLM预训练数据。通过结合文档级质量分类器、网络图中心性指标和Common Crawl的主机级覆盖率，发现约一半的分类器分数变异发生在主机间；少量页面采样即可估计主机剩余页面质量；Common Crawl使用的调和中心性选择主机效果有限，不如随机排序；基于采样的自适应深度爬取策略比均匀深度和中心性策略能以每次获取收集数倍的高质量页面；Common Crawl的主机覆盖率与质量信号相关性极低，每次爬取中仍有数千万高质量页面未被爬取，且集中在少数主机上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络爬取是LLM预训练数据的主要来源，但数据筛选流程会丢弃大量爬取页面。现有爬虫（如Common Crawl）的预算分配策略（基于调和中心性）是否真能高效收集高质量数据尚不明确，需要系统研究如何在主机间和主机内优化爬取预算分配，以提高高质量预训练数据的获取效率。

Method: 结合三类数据源：(1) 文档级质量分类器分数，(2) 网络图中心性指标（调和中心性等），(3) Common Crawl的主机级爬取覆盖率。在多个Common Crawl快照上进行模拟实验，对比不同主机选择策略（中心性 vs 随机）和主机内深度爬取策略（均匀深度、中心性加权、基于少量采样的自适应采样）的高质量页面收集效率，并分析主机间/主机内质量分数的变异分解及覆盖率与质量的相关性。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 质量分数约50%的变异来自主机间差异；(2) 每主机仅采样少量页面即可可靠估计其余页面质量；(3) 基于中心性选择主机相比随机选择增益有限，对某些分类器甚至更差；(4) 自适应采样策略（先少量采样，再向高分主机深度爬取）每次获取收集的高质量页面是均匀/中心性策略的数倍；(5) Common Crawl的主机覆盖率与质量信号几乎无相关；(6) 每次爬取中仍有数千万高质量页面未被爬取，高度集中于少数主机。

Conclusion: 当前Common Crawl基于中心性的爬取预算分配策略远非最优。应改用基于少量采样的自适应深度爬取策略：先跨主机广泛浅层采样，再将预算集中于高质量主机深度爬取。这能显著提高高质量预训练数据的获取效率，且已知未爬取URL中仍蕴藏大量高价值数据，主要集中在少数高质量主机上，为未来爬虫设计提供了明确的优化方向。

Abstract: Web crawling is a major source of large language model (LLM) pretraining data, yet data-selection pipelines discard a large share of crawled pages. We investigate how a crawler should allocate its budget across and within hosts to collect more high-quality pretraining data. Combining document-level quality classifier scores, web-graph centrality metrics, and Common Crawl's per-host crawl coverage, we find that roughly half of the variation in classifier scores occurs between hosts. In the crawls we analyze, a few pages from a host also provide a useful estimate of the quality of its remaining pages. Common Crawl uses harmonic centrality, a graph centrality metric, to guide its crawl allocation. In our simulations, using centrality to choose which hosts to crawl yields limited gains over random ordering and performs worse for some classifiers. For deciding how deeply to crawl each host, sampling a few pages and then fetching more from hosts with higher-scoring samples collects several times more high-quality pages per fetch than uniform depth or centrality-based allocation. Finally, Common Crawl's observed per-host coverage has little correlation with the evaluated quality signals. We estimate that tens of millions of high-quality pages remain among known but uncrawled URLs in each crawl examined, with most concentrated on a small share of hosts.

</details>

*Zijun Wang,Yifan Ji,Haidong Xin,Zhenghao Liu,Yukun Yan,Zulong Chen,Yu Gu,Ge Yu,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: T²Mem 是一种测试时记忆构建框架，将记忆构建推迟到推理阶段，在推理过程中自适应地逐步构建查询特定的记忆，显著降低构建开销并优于现有强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理长交互历史问答时表现不佳，相关证据分散于遥远会话中。现有记忆系统通常在观察用户查询前离线构建复杂记忆表示，不仅开销大，且无法根据查询的具体信息需求自适应调整记忆构建。

Method: 离线阶段提取轻量级的事件级元数据（关键事件、时序关联）作为检索线索，并保留指向原始交互的链接以便跨事件依赖重构。测试时，LLM 自适应检索并组织元数据与原始交互，在推理展开过程中渐进式构建查询特定记忆。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval 基准上，T²Mem 在不同骨干 LLM 上持续超越强基线，同时大幅降低记忆构建开销。进一步分析表明，将记忆构建推迟到推理时使过程能动态适应单个查询的信息需求。

Conclusion: T²Mem 通过测试时自适应记忆构建，解决了离线记忆系统开销大、缺乏查询自适应性的问题，实现了高效且动态的长上下文问答。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to answer questions over long interaction histories, where relevant evidence may be scattered across distant sessions. Existing memory systems typically construct and maintain sophisticated memory representations offline, before user queries are observed, incurring substantial overhead and limiting their ability to adapt memory construction to query-specific information needs. In this paper, we propose T$^2$Mem, a Test-Time Memory construction framework that defers memory construction to inference and progressively builds memory along the reasoning process. Specifically, T$^2$Mem extracts lightweight episode-level metadata offline to capture key events and temporal associations as retrieval cues, while retaining links to the original interactions to facilitate the reconstruction of dependencies across episodes. At test time, the LLM adaptively retrieves and organizes metadata and raw interactions, progressively constructing query-specific memory as reasoning unfolds. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval show the effectiveness of T$^2$Mem by consistently outperforming strong baselines across different backbone LLMs while substantially reducing the overhead of memory construction. Further analysis demonstrates that deferring memory construction to inference enables the memory construction process to dynamically adapt to the information needs of individual queries. All code is available at https://github.com/OpenBMB/T2Mem.

</details>


### [47] [Overcoming Data Imbalances with Expert Selection Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13370)
*Ayush K. Tarun,Clara Meister,Antoine Bosselut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于类别条件的专家选择偏置机制，用于解决MoE模型在数据不平衡情况下少数类别专家容量不足的问题。通过在输入类别基础上施加偏置，实现软性的类别对齐专业化，为低资源类别过量分配专家容量，在多模态、低资源语言和代码任务上取得显著性能提升，相当于额外增加18.5%-2.7%的训练token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽理论上支持专家按领域、语言或模态专业化，但数据不平衡导致少数类别集中在少数专家或低效分散，限制其可用容量。需要一种轻量级方法控制类别级专家分配，为低资源类别腾出空间同时保持标准MoE推理。

Method: 引入类别条件专家选择偏置：根据输入token的类别（如模态、语言、代码等）在路由阶段施加可学习或固定的偏置项，引导不同类别token优先路由至不同专家子集。这种软约束鼓励类别间专家分化，同时保留跨类别共享能力，并允许为数据稀缺类别过量分配专家容量。

Result: 在高达10.6B参数模型上验证：多模态、低资源语言和代码任务均获性能提升。最小模型规模增益相当于额外18.5%基线训练token，最大规模相当于2.7%。方法作为轻量级控制手段，有效改善类别级专家分配，无需修改推理流程。

Conclusion: 专家偏置机制为MoE模型提供了简单而有效的类别级容量控制手段，成功缓解数据不平衡导致的少数类别容量不足问题，在保持标准推理效率的同时显著提升低资源类别性能，具有广泛的实用价值。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have become the standard architecture for frontier LLMs. The multiple parallel experts in each transformer block theoretically enable expert specialization according to categories such as domains, languages, or modalities during pretraining. However, when data is imbalanced across categories, minority categories can become concentrated in a small fraction of the expert pool or spread inefficiently across them, effectively limiting their available capacity. In this work, we demonstrate that category-conditioned expert-selection biases can preferentially route tokens from different categories toward specialized expert pools. By applying biases based on input category, we provide a simple way to induce soft category-aligned specialization. This intervention encourages categories toward distinct expert subsets while retaining cross-category sharing when useful. More importantly, it enables the over-allocation of expert capacity to categories that would otherwise be disadvantaged by data scarcity. We observe performance improvements across modalities, low-resource languages, and code in models up to 10.6B parameters. At our smallest and largest model scales, these gains are equivalent to allocating 18.5% and 2.7% additional baseline training tokens, respectively. Overall, our results show that expert biases can serve as a lightweight control for category-level expert allocation, making room for low-resource categories while retaining standard MoE inference.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了评分标准设计的机制，发现结构化组织有助于更全面的标准构建，仅靠语义规范不足以保证有效的标准级判断，复杂的分数聚合增益有限。基于此提出ARTS-Hybrid方法，结合自适应树构建与语义优先、响应校准的细化，实验表明其提升了端到端评估性能和内在评分标准质量，且生成的评分标准能为模型优化提供有效信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注生成更好的评分标准，但对什么使评分标准设计有效的理解有限。本文旨在通过系统的机制分析，揭示评分标准设计中标准组织、标准操作化和分数聚合的关键因素，从而指导更有效的评分标准生成方法。

Method: 提出Adaptive Rubric Tree Search with Hybrid refinement (ARTS-Hybrid)，结合自适应树构建与语义优先、响应校准的细化策略。通过机制分析指导设计，实现评分标准的自适应生成和优化。

Result: 实验显示ARTS-Hybrid在多个基准上显著提升端到端评估性能和内在评分标准质量。进一步实验表明，其生成的评分标准能为语言模型优化提供有效信号。

Conclusion: 结构化组织是构建全面评分标准的关键，语义规范需配合响应校准，复杂分数聚合边际收益递减。ARTS-Hybrid通过自适应树搜索和混合细化有效解决了这些问题，不仅提升评估质量，还为模型优化提供了有价值的反馈信号。

Abstract: Rubrics have been widely used to evaluate and optimize language models on open-ended tasks. However, existing work primarily focuses on generating better rubrics, with limited understanding of what makes rubric design effective. In this work, we conduct a systematic mechanistic analysis of rubric design across criterion organization, criterion operationalization, and score aggregation. Our analysis shows that structural organization enables more comprehensive criterion construction, semantic specification alone does not ensure effective criterion-level judgments, and sophisticated score aggregation provides limited additional gains. Guided by these findings, we introduce Adaptive Rubric Tree Search with Hybrid refinement (ARTS-Hybrid), which combines adaptive tree construction with semantic-first, response-calibrated refinement. Experiments across benchmarks show that ARTS-Hybrid improves both end-to-end evaluation performance and intrinsic rubric quality. Further experiments suggest that, beyond evaluation, rubrics generated by ARTS-Hybrid provide effective signals for model optimization.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出MoPrO-SD，一种针对立场检测的提示优化框架，将提示分解为功能语义模块并顺序优化，引入三种优化策略（I、I+R、I+R+E）利用类别条件错误反馈，并在新基准VaccineStance上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在立场检测任务中因隐性观点、讽刺及上下文依赖表达而表现不佳；现有自动提示优化方法未针对立场标签语义及类别特定歧义设计，需开发专用优化框架。

Method: MoPrO-SD将提示分解为功能语义模块，采用LLM反馈顺序优化。研究三种策略：仅指令优化（I）、指令加推理（I+R）、指令加逐例推理（I+R+E），利用类别条件错误进行立场标签细化。同时构建人工标注的疫苗立场检测基准VaccineStance。

Result: 在四个数据集和五个开放权重LLM上，MoPrO-SD多数情况下优于人工基准提示。SemEval-2016上，类别条件反馈在五个主干中四个获得更高平均Macro-F1。I+R和I+R+E均表现强劲，相对效果随模型和数据集变化，与近期提示优化方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: MoPrO-SD通过模块化提示分解和类别条件反馈的顺序优化，有效提升LLM在立场检测上的表现，推理增强策略鲁棒性强，新基准VaccineStance为领域提供评测资源。

Abstract: Stance detection remains challenging for large language models (LLMs) due to implicit opinions, sarcasm, and context-dependent expressions of stance. Although automatic prompt optimization offers an alternative to fine-tuning without updating model parameters, existing structured approaches are not specifically designed around stance-label semantics and class-specific ambiguities. We introduce MoPrO-SD, a stance-specific prompt optimization framework that decomposes prompts into functional semantic modules and refines them sequentially using LLM-based feedback. We study three refinement strategies: instruction-only optimization (I), instruction with reasoning (I+R), and instruction with per-example reasoning (I+R+E), using class-conditioned errors for stance-label refinement. We also introduce VaccineStance, a new human-annotated benchmark for vaccine stance detection. Experiments across four datasets and five open-weight LLMs show that MoPrO-SD improves upon the human-written Base prompt in many settings. On SemEval-2016, class-conditioned feedback achieves higher mean Macro-F1 than pooled feedback for four of five backbones. Both reasoning-based strategies, I+R and I+R+E, achieve strong performance, with their relative effectiveness varying across models and datasets, while remaining competitive with recent prompt optimization methods. The MoPrO-SD code and VaccineStance dataset are available on GitHub.

</details>


### [50] [Towards the Roof of the World: NLP Progress in Tibetan Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13398)
*Xi Cao,Yijie Li,Yuan Sun,Shiwei Dong,Zeguang Huang,Nuo Qun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2004-2026年间藏语自然语言处理（NLP）的140篇文献，揭示了该领域快速增长、以中国为主导的全流水线研究范式，并指出了方言覆盖、负责任NLP、评价基准、跨社区可见性及资源开放等关键缺口，提出开放科学、共享标准与社区参与的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 藏语作为青藏高原约七百万人使用的语言，在NLP领域长期代表性不足。作者旨在通过首个同行评审的系统性文献计量调查，全面梳理藏语NLP的发展脉络、研究分布及资源现状，为其他全球南方低资源语言提供可参考的全流水线研究范式。

Method: 从ACL Anthology和DBLP收集2004-2026年藏语NLP相关140篇出版物，按会议/期刊类型、研究领域、第一作者单位国家、资源发布情况进行标注与分类统计，采用文献计量分析揭示时间演变、地理分布、研究覆盖面及资源开放度。

Result: 84%的出版物集中于2018年后，研究自底向上扩展至语料、分词、文字、语音、应用及大语言模型全流水线；中国机构主导87%的首作论文，覆盖全部25个研究领域，其他国家集中于古典藏语；仅29%论文发布数据/代码/模型，方言覆盖、责任NLP、原生评测、跨社区可见性等方面存在显著短板。

Conclusion: 藏语NLP在复杂文字、方言多样性及地理偏远的多重约束下形成的全流水线范式，可为其他低资源语言提供借鉴。未来需推动开放科学、制定共享标准、加强社区参与，以弥补资源发布不足、评测缺失及跨社区协作薄弱等问题，迎接大模型时代的挑战。

Abstract: Tibetan, spoken by some seven million people on and around the Qinghai-Xizang Plateau, remains underrepresented in natural language processing (NLP). We present, to our knowledge, the first peer-reviewed systematic bibliometric survey of Tibetan NLP: 140 publications from 2004 to 2026 in the ACL Anthology and DBLP, annotated for venue type, research area, first-affiliation country, and resource release. Output has grown rapidly, with 84% of the publications appearing since 2018, and has expanded bottom-up along the NLP pipeline, from corpora and word segmentation through script, speech, and applications to large language models (LLMs). Researchers based in China first-authored 87% of the publications, which span all 25 represented research areas, whereas work from other countries concentrates on Classical Tibetan. We argue that this full-pipeline paradigm, realized under the compounded constraints of a complex script, dialectal diversity, and geographic remoteness, offers a reference model for other low-resource languages of the Global South. We also identify gaps in dialect coverage, responsible NLP, native and comparable evaluation, cross-community visibility, and resource release (only 29% of the publications release data, code, or models), and recommend open science, shared standards, and community participation for the LLM era.

</details>


### [51] [Towards Enriching Design-Time Models with Evidence from Simulation and Operational Activities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13456)
*Barbara da Silva Oliveira,Julien Deantoni,Nicolas Ferry*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于证据的工作流，将仿真与运行阶段产生的知识系统地反馈到设计时模型中，以影响模型为例，在跟随标志机器人用例中验证了该工作流的可行性及其对设计时推理的改进作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络物理系统开发涉及多阶段建模活动，仿真、实验与运行阶段的执行观测可能揭示设计知识的缺失、不确定或失效，但从执行结果到设计时知识持久化的路径往往隐性不明，亟需系统化的知识反馈机制。

Method: 提出基于证据的工作流，利用影响模型作为设计时制品，通过仿真证据支持参与者剪枝决策、交互表征、影响函数抽象和有效性范围信息四个维度的模型丰富，以跟随标志机器人为用例演示。

Result: 实验表明该工作流在仿真证据驱动下可行，能有效丰富影响模型，改进设计时推理，并为后续开发迭代提供反馈。

Conclusion: 基于证据的工作流可系统化地将仿真与运行知识回迁至设计时模型，提升设计时推理质量，促进网络物理系统开发的迭代优化。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems are developed through multiple model based activities, comprising several stages of development. As the development progresses through simulation, experimentation, and operation, observations of system execution may reveal missing, uncertain, or invalid design knowledge. The path from execution results to persistent design-time enrichment, however, is often implicit. This paper addresses this challenge by proposing an evidence driven workflow for systematically transferring knowledge produced during simulation and operation back into design-time models. We demonstrate the workflow using an Influence Model, a design-time artefact used to represent how design artefacts and environmental factors jointly affect system response properties, using a sign-following robot use case. We show how simulation evidence can enrich Influence Models by supporting participant pruning decisions, interaction characterisation, influence-function abstraction, and validity-scope information. The application demonstrates the feasibility of the workflow for simulation-based evidence and its potential benefits to improve design-time reasoning and feedback in subsequent development iterations.

</details>


### [52] [A Better Metric Will Not Save You: What You Compare Beats How You Compare It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13476)
*Noureddine Khaous,Guillaume Wisniewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了表示相似性度量（RSA、CKA、Procrustes）对实验条件选择的敏感性，发现条件集合的构建方式（纳入哪些输入及如何分组）对模型排序影响巨大，甚至超过被测量效应本身的两倍，而条件数量变化则无影响。跨语言的模型排序不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表示相似性度量都假设条件集合是给定的，但条件集合由两个先验决定构成（纳入哪些输入、如何分组），没有不变性质能触及这些决定。作者想在已知真实排列结构（元音的声学组织）的情况下，量化这些先验选择对度量结果的影响。

Method: 使用15种语言和27种表示，在元音声学组织这一独立已知的真实结构下，系统地变化条件集合的构建方式（改变比较的类别、改变类别数量），测量不同表示相似性度量下的模型排序变化。

Result: 改变比较的类别（固定类别数量）导致两个表示间的差距变动超过被测量效应大小的两倍；改变类别数量则完全不影响差距。在特定条件选择下可以识别模型排序，但跨语言的模型排序不一致。

Conclusion: 表示相似性分析的结果高度依赖于实验条件的预先选择，这种依赖性远大于度量本身的差异。研究者在使用RSA、CKA等度量时，必须认真考虑并报告条件集合的构建依据，因为这决定了结论的有效性和可泛化性。

Abstract: Representational similarity measures (RSA, CKA, Procrustes) are chosen for the transformations they treat as irrelevant, and all of them take the set of conditions as given. That set is fixed by two decisions, which inputs are admitted and how they are grouped, both taken before the matrix a measure operates on exists: no invariance property reaches them. We measure what they are worth where the arrangement the conditions ought to have is known independently of the systems compared, the acoustic organisation of vowels, across fifteen languages and twenty-seven representations. Changing which categories are compared, at a fixed number of them, moves the gap between two representations by more than twice the effect being measured, while changing how many moves it not at all. In this setting rankings of models are identified under these choices, and rankings across languages are not.

</details>


### [53] [Proactive Agentic Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13485)
*Xueyang Feng,Chaozhuo Li,Xiaohe Bo,Jiakai Tang,Chenxi Li,Qiwei Ye,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 POrchestra，一种即时主动的代理编排框架，允许子代理在执行过程中主动请求调整工具/目标，并引入基于事后反馈的 On-Policy Orchestration Distillation (OPOD) 优化编排策略。在三个长时程基准上，任务成功率平均提升 9.1%，结合 OPOD 后小模型编排器再提升 10.0%，且成本-性能权衡更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理编排方法存在两大局限：执行时子代理的目标与工具在创建时固定，难以适应动态环境；优化时反馈稀疏且延迟，导致编排策略难以有效学习。

Method: 1. POrchestra 框架：子代理可在任意逻辑点主动与编排器通信请求工具/目标调整，保持本地执行连续性。2. OPOD 算法：将子代理的主动修正请求归因到先前的编排决策，生成针对性的过程监督信号，结合最终奖励联合优化编排策略。

Result: 在深度研究、动态数字助手、软件工程三个长时程基准上：POrchestra 相对最强基线平均提升任务成功率 9.1%；结合 OPOD 后，小编排器相对现有训练基线平均提升 10.0%；同时实现更优的成本-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 即时主动编排机制有效解决了动态环境下的适应性问题，基于事后反馈的过程监督显著提升了编排策略的学习效率，为长时程代理任务提供了可扩展、高性价比的解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic orchestration delegates tasks to sub-agents with independent contexts, providing a key path to extending the effective execution horizon of large language model agents. However, existing orchestration methods face two limitations: in execution, current methods fix sub-agents' goals and harnesses at creation time, limiting their adaptability to dynamic real-world environments; in optimization, sparse and delayed feedback constrains effective learning of the orchestration policy. To address these limitations, we introduce POrchestra, an on-the-fly proactive agentic orchestration framework. It allows sub-agents to proactively communicate with the orchestrator at any logical point to request harness adjustments while maintaining continuity of local execution. This mechanism not only enables sub-agents to adapt seamlessly to dynamic environments but also naturally inspires On-Policy Orchestration Distillation (OPOD) based on hindsight feedback. OPOD attributes sub-agents' proactive revision requests back to preceding orchestration decisions to derive targeted improvement signals, and leverages these signals as process supervision alongside the final reward to optimize the orchestration policy. We evaluate POrchestra on three long-horizon agent benchmarks spanning deep research, dynamic digital assistance, and software engineering. POrchestra improves task success by an average of 9.1% relative to the strongest baseline; combined with OPOD, it improves small-orchestrator success by an average of 10.0% relative to existing training baselines. POrchestra also achieves a more favorable cost-performance trade-off. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/XueyangFeng/POrchestra.

</details>


### [54] [Personalization Without Stereotyping: Do Speech LLMs Know When to Turn a Deaf Ear?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13550)
*Kshitij Ambilduke,Caroline Brun,Thibaut Thonet,Vassilina Nikoulina,Marcely Zanon Boito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 BASIS-Bench 基准，用于评估语音大语言模型在人口统计鲁棒性、刻板印象偏见和个性化三个维度的表现，实验发现模型虽能识别人口统计线索，但难以将其转化为恰当的个性化响应，显式标签虽能提升个性化却也会放大文本骨干中的刻板印象关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音大语言模型能利用丰富的副语言线索实现更复杂的个性化交互，但缺乏研究探讨这种敏感性如何导致刻板印象，现有评估主要测量人口统计差异，未区分有益的个性化与刻板印象行为。

Method: 构建 BASIS-Bench 基准，包含三个评估轴：(1) 人口统计鲁棒性，测量基于内容的任务在不同说话者和人口统计分类能力上的差异；(2) 刻板印象偏见，测量感知到的人口统计线索是否触发未经提示的刻板印象行为；(3) 个性化，测量模型在明确要求时是否能根据人口统计特征调整响应。对八个近期语音大语言模型进行评估。

Result: 尽管模型通常能识别人口统计线索，但大多数无法将其转化为适当的个性化。显式人口统计标签能改善个性化，但也会放大文本骨干中已存在的刻板印象关联。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了训练语音大语言模型不仅要感知人口统计线索，还要学会何时应让这些线索塑造其响应的必要性。

Abstract: Recent speech LLMs can leverage rich paralinguistic cues, enabling more complex and personalized interactions. However, little work investigates how this sensitivity can lead to stereotyping, and existing evaluations largely measure demographic disparities without distinguishing beneficial personalization from stereotypical behavior. We introduce BASIS-Bench, a benchmark designed to disentangle these two aspects through three axes: (1) demographic robustness, measuring disparities in content-based tasks across speakers and demographic classification capabilities; (2) stereotypical bias, measuring whether perceived demographic cues trigger unprompted stereotypical behavior; and (3) personalization, measuring whether models can tailor responses to demographic characteristics when explicitly requested. We evaluate eight recent speech LLMs and find that, despite often recognizing demographic cues, most fail to translate them into appropriate personalization. Explicit demographic labels improve personalization but also amplify stereotypical associations already present in the textual backbones. These findings highlight the need to train speech LLMs not only to perceive demographic cues, but to learn when they should shape their responses.

</details>


### [55] [Measuring Artifact Reliance in Machine-Generated Text Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13583)
*Anastasia Voznyuk,Andrey Grabovoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了机器生成文本检测器在科学文献基准上表现优异的主要原因是利用了PDF提取格式伪影（如人工文本保留原始换行，生成文本为单块文本）等表面特征，而非真实的语言差异。通过引入格式反事实测试和格式归一化，显著提升了检测器的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测器在科学文献基准上表现近乎完美，但研究者怀疑这可能源于基准构建方式引入的伪影信号，而非检测器真正捕捉了人机文本的本质差异。旨在识别并消除此类虚假信号，评估检测器的真实泛化能力。

Method: 1. 分析6个热门资源中的10个科学文档子集；2. 使用27个表面特征的逻辑回归作为基线；3. 引入'格式反事实'：仅重新换行机器生成摘要（不改词），测试检测器决策翻转率；4. 对比格式归一化前后的训练效果（域内/域外泛化）。

Result: 1. 逻辑回归仅用表面特征即达0.833-0.997宏F1，在arXiv子集上超越所有评测检测器（F1 0.951-0.997）；2. PDF提取伪影（换行差异）与3个格式特征结合达0.996 AUC；3. 格式反事实导致公开检测器20-27%正确决策翻转；4. 格式归一化将泛化鸿沟从0.32降至0.22，使域外宏F1从0.33升至0.65。

Conclusion: 当前基准中的高检测性能主要源于格式伪影而非语言本质。格式反事实可有效识别此类虚假信号，格式归一化能显著提升检测器的真实泛化能力。未来基准构建和检测器评测需控制格式混杂因素。

Abstract: Detectors of machine-generated text score almost perfectly on scientific-literature benchmarks, implying we can reliably distinguish with these detectors human and machine-generated documents. We looked at ten scientific-document subsets from six popular resources, and on several of them that apparent ease is a side effect of how the documents were put together. A logistic regression using 27 superficial features reached 0.833-0.997 macro-F1, and on the three arXiv subsets it outperformed every detector we evaluate with F1-scores 0.951-0.997. The strongest cue to the detector is the PDF extraction artifact: human texts retain their original line breaks, whereas generated texts are extracted as a single block of prose. This cue, together with three other formatting features, tell the classes apart at 0.996 AUC. To distinguish benchmarks that contain such a signal from detectors that rely on one, we introduce format counterfactuals: just re-wrapping a machine-written abstract without changing a single word reverses 20-27% of a public detector's correct decisions. Normalizing formatting before training reduces the generalization gap from 0.32 to 0.22. On format-clean benchmarks, it also raises out-of-domain macro-F1 from 0.33 to 0.65 for detectors trained on artifact-containing subsets.

</details>


### [56] [zip2zip++: Towards Inference-Time Token Compression with Minimal Quality Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13605)
*Saibo Geng,Andrea Gentilini,Xinxian Ma,Nathan Ranchin,Yunzhen Yao,Robert West*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过改进LZW压缩层和编解码器，在不修改预训练语言模型的情况下，大幅缩小动态词表压缩带来的推理和困惑度性能损失，实现了接近无损的自适应词表压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LZW的动态词表压缩虽能减少token数量，但会显著降低推理能力和困惑度表现。作者旨在证明这种质量损失并非压缩的必然代价，而是可通过针对性优化来弥补。

Method: 针对四大核心问题提出解决方案：(1) 设计解耦的输入/输出动态嵌入层，消除共享嵌入无法解决的隐式冲突；(2) 引入大小感知的RoPE位置编码，正确处理合并token跨越多个原始token的情况；(3) 修改LZW编解码器支持选择性合并和多视角分段，避免数字等关键token被合并导致下游任务准确率下降；(4) 通过多视角分段缓解动态词表固有的分段歧义对评测的干扰。

Result: 在四个模型上，该方法在所有评测语料上均提升困惑度，数学推理准确率显著提高，在相似压缩率下弥补了zip2zip与未压缩基线约一半的质量差距。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过压缩层和编解码器的改进可实现近无损的自适应词表压缩，为大语言模型的高效推理提供了重要进展。

Abstract: Dynamic vocabularies built on-the-fly via Lempel-Ziv-Welch (LZW) compression let a language model represent the same text in fewer tokens, but prior work shows that this efficiency comes at a cost to reasoning and perplexity. We show that this quality gap is not a fundamental price of compression but can be substantially closed through targeted improvements to the compression layer and LZW codec, without modifying the underlying pretrained language model. Our analysis reveals four key issues: (1) LZW's compression algorithm creates an implicit conflict between the input embedding and the output embedding, a tension that a single shared embedding cannot resolve; (2) naively applying RoPE to LZW-compressed sequences ignores that merged tokens can span multiple tokens; (3) merging certain tokens (notably digits) disproportionately hurts downstream accuracy; and (4) dynamic vocabularies introduce an inherent segmentation ambiguity that confounds standard evaluation. We address these through decoupled input/output dynamic embedding layer, size-aware RoPE positioning, and a modified LZW codec supporting selective merges and multi-view segmentations. Across four models, our recipe improves perplexity on every corpus we evaluated as well as mathematical reasoning accuracy, closing roughly half the quality gap between zip2zip and the uncompressed baseline at a similar compression ratio on general text. This represents a significant step toward lossless adaptive-vocabulary compression for language models.

</details>


### [57] [BLiMPPI: Benchmark of Linguistic Minimal Pairs for Polarity Items](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13607)
*Arzu Burcu Güven,Anna Rogers,Leonie Weissweiler,Joakim Nivre,Rob van der Goot*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了BLiMPPI，一个包含33种语言中12,000多个NPI最小对的多语言评估套件，通过从Universal Dependencies树库自动发现NPI并构建最小对，评估了12个多语言和2个单语模型，发现多语言模型在低资源语言和非n-gram可解的语境中表现最差，揭示了当前模型在处理跨语言NPI许可现象上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负极性项目（NPIs）如'anymore'和'ever'仅在特定语境（主要是否定语境）中合法，其语法性同时依赖句法和语义，在理论语言学中有丰富的多语言研究，但语言模型方面的研究几乎局限于英语。论文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估多语言模型在多种语言中对NPI许可约束的捕捉能力。

Method: 构建BLiMPPI评估套件：利用Universal Dependencies（UD）树库自动发现NPI，标注其许可环境，并通过扰动生成最小对。套件覆盖33种语言，含12,000+最小对。评估12个多语言模型（如mBERT、XLM-R等）和2个单语模型，并与n-gram基线对比。进一步分析NPI类型、语系、许可变异性及NPI-许可词距离的影响。

Result: 多语言模型在低资源语言上表现显著较差；在无法通过n-gram统计解决的最小对上困难最大。模型性能受NPI类型、语系、许可环境变异性及NPI与许可词距离影响。n-gram基线在部分语言上表现竟优于多语言模型，表明模型未充分学习句法-语义许可约束。

Conclusion: BLiMPPI为多语言NPI评估提供了大规模基准，揭示了当前多语言模型在捕捉跨语言NPI许可约束上的系统性不足，尤其在低资源语言和需要深层句法-语义推理的场景下。未来工作需改进模型对许可约束的建模能力，并扩展至更多语言现象。

Abstract: Negative polarity items (NPIs), such as "anymore" and "ever", are grammatical only in select environments, predominantly in the context of negation. Because their grammaticality depends on both syntax and semantics, they have a rich, multilingual literature in theoretical linguistics, yet work on NPIs in language models is almost entirely confined to English. We introduce BLiMPPI, a multilingual evaluation suite of over 12,000 NPI minimal pairs in 33 languages, built by a pipeline that discovers NPIs in Universal Dependencies (UD) treebanks, labels their licensing environments, and perturbs these into minimal pairs. Evaluating 12 multilingual and 2 monolingual models alongside n-gram baselines, we find that multilingual models struggle most with lower-resource languages and with pairs that are not n-gram solvable. We further examine the effects of NPI typology, language family, licensing variability, and NPI-licenser distance. Data: https://github.com/arzuburcuguven/blimppi-data

</details>


### [58] [Training User Simulators with Latent Intrinsic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13617)
*Tarek Naous,Craig Boutilier,Wei Xu,Ofer Meshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出带有内在反馈的用户模拟器，通过潜在评估状态（内部思考、反应、标量评分）从第一人称视角逐轮评估助手，利用真实交互日志训练，显著提升模拟真实度、评论行为及逐轮评估能力，其生成的评估与人类判断相关性更高，且作为多轮RL训练的奖励信号时可匹配外部裁判LLM效果并将计算量减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟器主要关注生成真实用户话语，未探索其作为助手响应直接反馈源的潜力；此类评估信号往往不显性表达于语言中，而是潜藏于用户的隐性推理中。

Method: 构建具备内在反馈的LLM用户模拟器，引入潜在评估状态（内部思考、反应、标量评分）在每轮对话中从第一人称视角评估助手；利用真实用户与助手的交互日志训练模拟器。

Result: 内在反馈显著提升模拟真实度与评论行为，提供现有微调模拟器缺乏的逐轮评估；生成的评估与人类判断相关性优于同规模提示式模拟器；作为多轮RL奖励信号时，效果匹配外部裁判LLM且将RL计算量减少过半。

Conclusion: 带内在反馈的用户模拟器能有效提供逐轮评估信号，提升模拟质量与评估准确性，并为多轮RL训练提供高效奖励，大幅降低计算成本，为LLM助手训练提供可扩展的自动化评估范式。

Abstract: User simulators are gaining attention as environments to train LLM assistants that can better cope with the nuances of multi-turn conversations with real users. Prior work on user simulation has emphasized generating realistic user utterances, leaving unexplored their potential as a source of direct feedback on the assistant's responses. Such evaluation signals may not be explicitly communicated in language, but can instead reside in the latent reasoning of users. In this work, we introduce user simulators with intrinsic feedback, namely, models equipped with latent evaluation states: internal thoughts, reactions, and scalar ratings that evaluate the assistant from a first-person user perspective at each conversation turn. We train LLM-based simulators using interaction logs between real users and assistants and demonstrate that intrinsic feedback improves simulation realism and critiquing behaviors versus prompted simulator baselines, while also providing turn-level evaluations that existing fine-tuned simulators lack. Further, evaluations generated by our trained simulators correlate better with human judgments than those of prompted simulators of the same size. Lastly, our experiments reveal that simulators with intrinsic feedback provide effective reward signals for multi-turn RL training, matching setups that rely on external judge LLMs for rewards while cutting RL compute by more than half.

</details>


### [59] [AMLworld-Compact: Importance-Weighted Downsampling for Cost-Effective LLM Evaluation and Error Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13627)
*Natapong Nitarach,Phume Ngampornsukswadi,Pittawat Taveekitworachai,Surapon Nonesung,Warit Sirichotedumrong,Kunat Pipatanakul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出AMLworld-Compact评估集，通过HT-Coreset协议大幅减少评估目标（271x和611x），利用Horvitz-Thompson加权提供无偏全测试混淆矩阵计数估计，实现高效且无偏的LLM评估，并发布评估集、构建代码和审计工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AMLworld测试集上评估大语言模型成本高昂，因为超过百万目标每个都需要单独的图提示；虽然采样可降低成本，但小型平衡子集会改变无权重精确度和F1指标，需要一种既能保持评估效率又能提供无偏指标估计的方法。

Method: 1. 构建AMLworld-Compact：保留所有非法测试交易，从监督评分分层中采样良性交易。2. HT-Coreset协议：在HI-Small和LI-Small上分别将评估目标减少271倍和611倍。3. Horvitz-Thompson加权：为固定预测提供无偏全测试混淆矩阵计数估计，召回率精确，精确度和F1为比率估计。4. 使用图特征预处理器(GFP)特征，一个提升树家族定义层评估另一个家族，进行500次抽样实验。5. 对1,000个HI-Small few-shot输出进行结果分层审计，分析词汇检查通过率。

Result: 1. 评估目标大幅减少：HI-Small减少271倍，LI-Small减少611倍。2. F1 RMSE为0.00-2.58个百分点，显著优于带HT权重均匀采样的4.44-7.41个百分点，但保守区间仍较宽。3. 七个开放权重LLMs在评估图提示下的加权精确度接近全预测非法基线。4. 词汇检查对正确二元判决通过率84.5%，错误判决通过率56.2%，表明这些标记无法确立正确性。

Conclusion: AMLworld-Compact提供了一个高效、无偏的LLM评估框架，大幅降低评估成本同时保持指标估计准确性。发布的评估集、构建代码和审计工具促进了反洗钱领域LLM评估的标准化。但需注意保守置信区间仍较宽，且简单词汇标记不足以验证模型输出正确性。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) on AMLworld's smallest test splits is costly because each of over a million targets requires a separate graph prompt. Sampling reduces this cost, but small balanced subsets change unweighted precision and F1. We introduce AMLworld-Compact, an evaluation set that retains every illicit test transaction and samples benign transactions from supervised-score strata. Its HT-Coreset protocol reduces evaluation targets by 271x and 611x on HI-Small and LI-Small. Horvitz-Thompson weights give unbiased full-test confusion-count estimates for fixed predictions; recall is exact, while precision and F1 are ratio estimates. In a held-out study using Graph Feature Preprocessor (GFP) features, one boosted-tree family defines strata for evaluating the other over 500 draws per configuration. At the released sizes, F1 RMSE is 0.00-2.58 percentage points versus 4.44-7.41 for uniform sampling with HT weights; conservative intervals remain broad. Under the evaluated graph prompts, seven open-weight LLMs have weighted precision near the predict-all-illicit baseline. In an outcome-stratified audit of 1,000 HI-Small few-shot outputs from one run per model, lexical checks pass for 84.5% of correct and 56.2% of incorrect binary verdicts, so these markers do not establish correctness. We release the evaluation sets, construction code, and audit tools at https://huggingface.co/datasets/typhoon-ai/AMLworldCompactEval and https://github.com/nat-nischw/AMLworld-Compact.

</details>


### [60] [Multi-Stream Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13630)
*Xiaohan Qin,Xiangdong Zhang,Tuo Dai,Sunan Zou,Huaijin Wu,Yebin Yang,Zhuo Xia,Lin Yao,Yuliang Liu,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出多流注意力残差 (MSAR)，将单流注意力残差 (AttnRes) 扩展为多头深度注意力机制，通过多尺度因果卷积从序列上下文获取流多样性，经自适应混合生成单流层输入，在 18B 和 28B MoE 规模上显著超越标准残差、mHC 和 AttnRes，实现 1.58× 等损失计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AttnRes 实现了沿深度轴的单流 softmax 注意力，但受限于单流表达能力。受序列轴多头注意力成功启发，作者希望将深度注意力推广为多头机制，以增强历史表示检索的多样性，同时避免持久多流存储和通信开销。

Method: MSAR 通过多尺度因果卷积从序列上下文提取多样化流，不同头以不同.context 视角检索历史表示，再经轻量级自适应混合合并为单流层输入。设计保留单流源并在需要时按需扩展，无需持久多流存储/通信。

Result: 在 12 个下游基准上，MSAR 在 18B 和 28B MoE 规模均持续优于标准残差、mHC 和 AttnRes。Scaling-law 实验显示等损失计算加速达 1.58×（标准残差基线），AttnRes 为 1.35×，mHC 为 1.30×。

Conclusion: MSAR 以极小额外开销实现多头深度注意力，兼顾性能提升与工程效率，是大规模预训练极具前景的残差架构。

Abstract: Attention Residuals (AttnRes) enables content-dependent retrieval over network depth, which can be viewed as single-stream softmax attention along the depth axis. Inspired by the success of multi-head attention along the sequence axis, we introduce Multi-Stream Attention Residuals (MSAR), an extension of AttnRes that generalizes this mechanism to multi-head depth attention, yielding substantial performance gains with minimal additional overhead. MSAR draws stream diversity from sequence context through multi-scale causal convolutions, allowing different heads to retrieve historical representations through distinct contextual views while preserving the integrity of the hidden representation. The retrieved streams are combined through lightweight adaptive mixing to generate the single-stream layer input. By retaining single-stream sources and expanding them on demand, MSAR supports multi-stream computation without requiring persistent multi-stream storage or communication. Across all 12 downstream benchmarks at the 18B and 28B MoE scales, MSAR consistently outperforms prior residual variants, including standard residual connections, manifold-constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC), and AttnRes. Scaling-law experiments yield an iso-loss compute speedup of $1.58\times$ over the standard residual baseline, compared with $1.35\times$ for AttnRes and $1.30\times$ for mHC. These results make MSAR a promising residual architecture for large-scale pre-training.

</details>


### [61] [Inject a Turn, Not a Token: Learning Where to Redirect LLM Reasoning for Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13672)
*Jiwon Jeon,Myungsik Cho,Jeonghye Kim,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 UPTURN，一种无需微调大语言模型的推理纠错框架。通过在冻结编码器上训练轻量级 MLP 头预测干预位置，并将纠错指令作为新用户轮次注入而非内联注入，实现推理重定向。实验显示其在多个推理器和数学基准上均优于基线，且具备跨模型迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型难以可靠地自我识别和纠正推理错误。现有基于训练的方法需微调模型生成修订或反馈，成本高且泛化受限。本文旨在开发一种免微调、可插拔的纠错框架，仅利用最终答案正确性信号学习干预位置，并通过对话轮次注入实现有效重定向。

Method: 1. 训练 13 万参数的 MLP 头接在冻结编码器上，仅以最终答案正确性为监督，预测在失败推理轨迹中何处干预。2. 发现固定生成预算下，内联指令注入难以重定向推理，但将相同指令作为新用户轮次呈现能有效改变模型推理路径。3. UPTURN 流程：保留选定前缀，注入纠错指令为新用户轮次，重新生成后缀。无需微调 LLM。

Result: 在四个推理器和六个数学基准上，UPTURN 在每种设置下平均准确率均超越 32 轮基线。在匹配纠错预算下优于自我纠正和反思令牌注入基线。无需重新训练即可跨推理器迁移并泛化至 GPQA-Diamond。可与 GRPO 和自我纠正方法互补结合。

Conclusion: UPTURN 证明了通过轻量级位置预测器和对话轮次注入机制，可在不修改模型参数的情况下显著提升 LLM 推理纠错能力。该方法具有高效性、通用性和可组合性，为免微调的推理增强提供了实用范式。

Abstract: Large language models struggle to reliably identify and correct errors in their own reasoning. Unlike prior training-based approaches that fine-tune an LLM to generate revisions or feedback, we learn where to intervene in failed rollouts and determine how to redirect them. To choose where to intervene, we train a 130K-parameter MLP head on a frozen encoder using only final-answer correctness. For redirection, we find that under a fixed generation budget, the tested inline injections fail to reliably redirect reasoning, whereas presenting the same instruction in a new user turn changes the model's course. The resulting correction framework, UPTURN (User-Prompt TURN injection at a learned position), requires no LLM fine-tuning: it retains a selected prefix, injects an instruction as a new user turn, and regenerates the suffix. Across four reasoners and six math benchmarks, UPTURN improves average accuracy in every setting over a 32-rollout baseline. It outperforms self-correction and reflection-token injection baselines under matched correction budgets, transfers across reasoners and to GPQA-Diamond without retraining, and complements GRPO and self-correction methods.

</details>


### [62] [Retractions Revisited: A Replication, Extension, and Mechanistic Evaluation of Linguistic Obfuscation Effects in Fraudulent Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13678)
*David M. Markowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究复制并扩展了既有发现，比较了800篇造假论文与800篇真实论文（2013-2025年），证实造假论文的语言混淆程度更高；通过平行中介模型发现，现实监控（形容词使用率降低）最能解释造假与语言混淆的关联；基于混淆特征的交叉验证检测准确率为60.7%，基于100+语言维度的弹性网络模型准确率达71.6%，均显著优于随机猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明造假科学家写作时使用更高比例的语言混淆，可能机制是混淆增加读者分析成本，但该结果在开放科学时代缺乏实证审视，且未评估其他心理机制。本研究旨在复制并扩展这一发现，同时检验竞争性心理机制。

Method: 收集2013-2025年间的800篇造假论文与800篇匹配的真实对照论文，计算语言混淆指标；采用平行中介模型检验三种竞争机制（包括现实监控、认知负荷、情感距离等）；使用交叉验证评估基于混淆特征及基于100+语言维度的弹性网络模型的造假检测准确率。

Result: 结果支持语言混淆假设：造假论文显著更混淆。平行中介模型显示，现实监控（通过形容词使用率降低测量）最能解释造假与混淆的联系。检测准确率：混淆特征模型60.7%，弹性网络模型71.6%，均显著高于随机水平。

Conclusion: 造假论文表现出更高的语言混淆，主要归因于现实监控缺陷（形容词减少）。基于语言特征的自动化检测具备实用潜力，为科学造假识别及心理语言学研究提供新证据。

Abstract: Prior work suggests fraudulent scientists write their articles with higher rates of linguistic obfuscation than genuine scientists, with a possible mechanism suggesting obfuscation makes research more costly for a reader to analyze. This result has not undergone additional empirical scrutiny in the open science era, nor have other psychological mechanisms been evaluated. The current replication and extension addressed this opportunity by comparing 800 fraudulent and 800 genuine control papers (2013-2025). The results supported the linguistic obfuscation hypothesis, suggesting fraudulent papers were more obfuscated than genuine ones. A parallel mediation model evaluated three competing mechanisms, finding that reality monitoring, as measured through decreased rates of adjectives, best explained the link between fraudulent science and linguistic obfuscation. Cross-validated detection accuracy using obfuscation features (60.7%) and an elastic net of over 100 language dimensions (71.6%) were significantly greater than chance. Implications for scientific fraud and psychology of language research are discussed.

</details>


### [63] [Verifiable Retrieval for Legal Reasoning through Structure-driven Knowledge Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13695)
*Ibrahim Siddig,Sviatoslav Tugeev,Munir Georges*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对可靠性关键领域（如法律）中LLM黑盒问题，提出基于结构驱动解析构建知识图谱（KG）的RAG增强方法，在德国法律法典上对比多种KG感知的分块与检索策略，评估其对引用准确率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的系统因黑盒特性无法满足医疗、法律等高可靠性场景对透明性和可验证性的要求；虽然RAG通过检索相关文档降低幻觉风险，但随着源文档数量和规模增加，朴素检索方法性能下降，易忽略关键上下文。

Method: 采用结构驱动解析为德国法律法典构建知识图谱，利用KG进行源文档导航与检索；设计并对比多种KG感知的分块（chunking）与检索策略，实验评估不同策略对检索引用准确率的影响。

Result: 实验表明，KG感知的分块与检索策略能有效提升大规模法律文档检索的引用准确率，优于朴素检索方法，验证了结构化知识图谱在增强RAG可靠性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于知识图谱的结构化检索方法能显著改善RAG在法律等高可靠性领域的表现，为构建透明、可验证的LLM应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Any automation within reliability-critical contexts such as medicine or law has to be transparent and verifiable. Despite their advantages, current LLM-based systems do not meet these requirements due to their inherent black-box nature. Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) attempts to ground the generative process in the relevant interpretable documents, thereby reducing the risk of AI 'hallucinations' and potentially enabling verification of the initial generation context. However, as the number and size of potential sources increases, naive retrieval approaches tend to perform worse, ignoring critical context elements. In this paper we aim to tackle this problem in the context of German Legal Code. Using structure-driven parsing to create a Knowledge Graph (KG) for the source document navigation and retrieval, we compare several KG-informed chunking and retrieval strategies and evaluate their effect on the accuracy of retrieved citations.

</details>


### [64] [Enhancing Legal Reasoning in Pre-trained Language Models via Knowledge Graph-Guided Multi-Task Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13722)
*Ibrahim Siddig,Munir Georges*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多任务预训练方法，用于德国交通法规(StVO)，结合NSP、KG引导聚类和KG感知段落分类三个目标，将结构化法律知识编码进模型权重，实现零样本法律推理，在多个基准上显著提升性能，并能迁移至奥地利驾驶法规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律语言模型仅依赖掩码语言建模，将法规文本视为扁平token序列，忽略了层级结构约束，无法有效进行半结构化法规文档的推理。

Method: 针对德国交通法规(StVO)设计多任务预训练框架，融合三个互补目标：下一句预测(NSP)、知识图谱引导聚类(CLU)和知识图谱感知段落分类(CLS)，显式将结构化法律知识编码入模型参数，推理时无需显式图遍历即可实现零样本法律推理。

Result: 在九种Transformer编码器和324次实验中，合成StVO问答任务上相对基线提升10.3%-155.0%；在LexGLUE七项法律基准中有四项超越先前最佳结果；在奥地利驾驶法规上实现3.4%-15.8%的零样本迁移增益。

Conclusion: 该多任务预训练方法成功将结构化法律知识内化于模型，不仅在源域德国交通法规上大幅提升推理性能，还能零样本泛化至相关法律领域(如奥地利法规)，验证了显式建模层级结构对法律语言理解的有效性。

Abstract: Legal language understanding requires reasoning over semi-structured regulatory documents, yet standard pre-training objectives do not explicitly target this structural capability. Because current legal language models rely solely on masked language modeling, they treat statutory text as flat token sequences and ignore the underlying hierarchical constraints. To bridge this gap, we present a multi-task pre-training approach for German traffic law (StVO). Our approach combines three complementary objectives: Next Sentence Prediction (NSP), Knowledge Graph (KG) Guided Clustering (CLU), and Knowledge Graph (KG) Aware Paragraph Classification (CLS). This approach explicitly encodes structured legal knowledge directly into the model weights. This encoding enables zero-shot legal reasoning at inference time and removes the need for discrete graph traversal. We evaluate our approach across nine Transformer-based encoders and 324 experimental runs. On synthetic StVO question answering, our models demonstrate consistent relative improvements between 10.3% and 155.0% over baselines. Furthermore, relative to the established LexGLUE baselines (Chalkidis et al., 2022), our models improve over prior results on four of seven legal benchmarks. It also achieves consistent zero-shot transfer gains between 3.4% and 15.8% on Austrian driving regulations. These experimental results indicate that our training approach generalizes successfully beyond the initial training legal domain.

</details>


### [65] [Not Just an Evaluation: A Case Study of Discourse Particle Sensitivity in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13794)
*William Sheffield,Jiahui Chen,Amy Zhang,Alex Warstadt,Junyi Jessy Li,Kanishka Misra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型对英语话语标记 'just' 和 'only' 的敏感性，发现模型与人类在偏好上相似，指令微调增强敏感性，且模型内部对两词的表示按词义部分组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 话语标记虽在自然语言中高频出现且微妙影响语篇流向，但因效果细微长期未被充分研究；现有工作缺乏对 LLM 对此类多义功能词敏感性的系统性行为与机制分析。

Method: 采用行为实验与机制分析相结合：1) 对比 LLM 与人类在语境中对含/不含 'just'/'only' 话语的偏好；2) 通过采样评估模型对生成文本似然的打分差异；3) 定位模型内部对两词敏感的神经单元，分析其跨词义迁移模式。

Result: 1) LLM 与人类均偏好含标记话语，且对 'just' 比 'only' 更敏感；2) 指令微调一致提升敏感性；3) 'only' 敏感单元选择性迁移至 'just' 的排他用法，而 'just' 所有单元同等迁移至 'only'，揭示词汇级表示按词义部分组织。

Conclusion: LLM 捕捉到了话语标记的语用功能与多义性结构，其内部表示呈现不对称的词义组织模式，指令微调能强化此类语用敏感性；发布的数据与代码为后续研究提供资源。

Abstract: Discourse particles subtly shape the flow of discourse by reflecting conversational context and interlocutor attitudes, often leaving the truth-conditional meaning untouched. They are also often polyfunctional, with distinct senses which partially overlap with other particles; for example, the exclusive use of English "just" and "only" ("John only/just drinks water"). Despite the frequency of these words in natural language, they have gone largely unstudied due to the subtle nature of their effects. We investigate LLM sensitivity to "just" and "only" in natural conversations using behavioral and mechanistic analyses. First, we find that LLMs and humans similarly prefer the presence of these particles in context, and both are more sensitive to "just" compared to "only". Second, when sampling from LLMs with and without these particles, we find LLMs consistently rate generations as more likely, given their source context, demonstrating their particle sensitivity. In both experiments, instruction tuning increases sensitivity. Finally, we localize particle-sensitive units within LLMs and find the overlap between "just" and "only" is asymmetric: "only" units transfer selectively to exclusive "just", while all "just" units transfer to "only" equally well regardless of sense, suggesting LLM lexical-level particle representations are partially organized by sense. We release our code, sense-labeled dialogue stimuli, and human preference annotations at https://github.com/JiahuiKChen/Not_Just_an_Evaluation.

</details>


### [66] [REFLECT: Role-Aware Detection of Sentiment, Emotion, Politeness, and Formality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13797)
*Sangpil Youm,Annabel Stocks Natalias,Hayden Heninger,Leah Jones,Xiaoyang Jin,Xiaoya Zhang,Bonnie Dorr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种角色感知的Transformer架构，将情感、情绪、礼貌、正式度（SEPF）四个维度映射到基于语义角色标注（SRL）的配置，并将其直接融入自注意力机制，实现了对对话中交际意义的更好检测，并在基准数据集和真实心理健康数据上取得了显著提升，同时提供了可追踪的、基于角色的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖上下文语言表示，而忽略了参与者角色结构在表达情感、情绪、礼貌、正式度等交际意义中的作用，语义角色被隐性处理，限制了模型对复杂交际现象的捕捉能力。

Method: 引入角色感知Transformer架构，将SEPF四个维度映射到理论奠基的SRL配置，并将这些配置直接融入Transformer的自注意力机制中。非方向性角色感知注意力建模内部状态（情感、情绪），方向性角色感知注意力捕捉发起者到接收者的关系（礼貌、正式度）。通过学习门控机制将角色感知表示与上下文嵌入融合，自适应加权角色信息的贡献，而非简单拼接辅助特征向量。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，该框架在情绪、礼貌、正式度检测上相对强BERT基线取得统计显著提升，增益集中在低频复杂情绪和高度礼貌表达等理论预测案例；情感检测提升不显著，表明情感更多通过词汇选择而非角色结构传递。真实心理健康数据评估显示，框架同时提升预测性能和模型可追踪性。

Conclusion: 基于语言学奠基的Transformer自注意力修改能改善交际意义检测，泛化至真实心理健康数据，并提供可追踪、基于角色的解释，超越预测性能实现可解释性。

Abstract: Detecting communicative meaning in dialogue requires modeling not only lexical content but also the participant-role structures through which sentiment, emotion, politeness, and formality (SEPF) are expressed. Existing approaches rely on contextual language representations while leaving semantic roles implicit. We introduce a role-aware transformer architecture that maps the four SEPF dimensions to theory-grounded Semantic Role Labeling (SRL) configurations, incorporating these configurations directly into the transformer's self-attention mechanism. Role-aware attention models non-directional internal states (sentiment and emotion), while directional role-aware attention captures initiator-to-recipient relations for external cues (politeness and formality). The resulting role-aware representations are fused with contextual embeddings through a learned gate that adaptively weights how much role information each input contributes, rather than appending semantic roles as an auxiliary feature vector. Across four benchmark datasets, the framework yields statistically reliable gains over strong BERT-based baselines for emotion, politeness, and formality, with gains concentrated in theoretically predicted cases such as low-frequency, complex emotions and highly polite expressions; sentiment improvements are marginal and non-significant, suggesting that sentiment is conveyed more through lexical choice than participant-role structure. Additional evaluation on real-world data demonstrates that the framework improves both predictive performance and model traceability. These results demonstrate that linguistically grounded modifications to transformer self-attention improve the detection of communicative meaning, generalize beyond benchmark datasets to real-world mental-health data, and yield traceable, role-grounded explanations that extend beyond predictive performance to interpretability.

</details>


### [67] [Error Rate is Not Enough: Costs of Augmentation in Low-Resource ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13807)
*Katsumi Ibaraki,David Chiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了低资源自动语音识别（ASR）中数据增强方法的互补效应，发现SpecAugment与基于文本的增强方法在声学属性和训练曝光度上互补，组合后产生超加性改进，但仅限于低资源场景（<100小时）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低资源ASR数据增强研究多关注聚合错误率，缺乏对增强方法为何及何时有效的深入分析，特别是不同增强方法在音素级错误类型上的差异及其互补机制。

Method: 在四种低资源语言和英语的五个数据量级上，交叉对比两类音频修改增强（语速扰动、SpecAugment）与两类文本修改合成增强，从音素层面分析错误类型，并关联声学属性（单一声学线索）与训练曝光度（词频分布）。

Result: SpecAugment虽带来最稳健的聚合改进，但增加声调和腭音化错误；文本增强引入辅音插入错误。两者结合可相互抵消对方引入的错误，声调和腭音化错误因训练曝光度最低（单一声学线索），经文本增强扁平化词频后频率提升37-38%而得到逆转。组合改进呈超加性，但在100小时数据量时消失。

Conclusion: 增强方法的选择应基于可预测的训练曝光度和声学属性（均可在训练前测量），而非盲目组合；低资源场景下利用互补性可获超加性收益，大规模数据下互补性消失。

Abstract: Data augmentation is standard practice for low-resource automatic speech recognition (ASR), but its benefits are almost always reported as aggregate error rates, leaving open why and when a method helps. On four low-resource languages and English at five data sizes, we cross two augmentations that modify existing audio (speed perturbation and SpecAugment) with two that modify text transcriptions and synthesize audio from it. We analyze errors at the phoneme level and find that they help for complementary reasons. SpecAugment has the most reliable aggregate improvements, but increases tone and palatalization errors, whereas text-based methods introduce consonant insertions; in combination, each reduces the errors the other introduces. This complementarity is predictable. Tone and palatalization are each signaled by a single acoustic property and occur least frequently in training (lowest training exposure), and text-based methods that flatten word frequency raise their frequency by 37--38%, reversing those errors while further improving aggregate error. The improvement from combining the methods exceeds the sum of their individual improvements; the superadditivity vanishes by 100 hours, limiting the complementarity to the low-resource regime. Where specific error types matter, we recommend selecting augmentation by training exposure and acoustic properties, both measurable before any training.

</details>


### [68] [Can Language Models Learn When to Abstain? Policy Learning and Calibrated Answer Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13821)
*Juan Terven,Diana-Margarita Córdova-Esparza,Ruben Gomez,Carlos Miranda Rocha,Julio-Alejandro Romero-González,Edgar Chavez-Urbiola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何改进语言模型的回答决策能力（何时回答、何时拒答），对比了训练模型自身与训练独立答案选择器两种方法，在Qwen3-14B上实验发现：学习型答案选择器优于原模型的校准置信度过滤器，在TriviaQA上覆盖率从20.2%提升至30.9%，错误率从23.0%降至14.5%；但强化学习方法无稳定增益，且在NQ-Open上仍不如直接拒答，跨数据集的可靠拒答问题仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型需具备判断何时应回答、何时应拒答的能力，现有方法主要依赖模型自身的置信度校准，但效果有限。论文旨在探索通过训练独立的答案选择器，或直接训练模型自身（如强化学习），来改进这种决策能力，并通过奖励正确回答、惩罚错误、允许拒答的评分机制进行量化评估。

Method: 在Qwen3-14B基础上，针对事实类问答任务，设计包含奖励正确、惩罚错误、允许拒答的效用函数。对比两种方法：1）训练独立的答案选择器；2）通过强化学习直接优化模型自身。在TriviaQA和NQ-Open数据集上，跨三个训练种子和两个置信度阈值评估阈值平均效用，并进行人工审计验证。

Result: 学习型答案选择器在TriviaQA上显著优于校准置信度过滤器：覆盖率从20.2%提升至30.9%，汇总错误率从23.0%降至14.5%（精确匹配评分）。强化学习方法未带来稳定增益。在NQ-Open上，选择器虽优于过滤器，但仍劣于始终拒答策略。人工审计确认TriviaQA结果可靠。

Conclusion: 在该实验设定下，训练独立的答案选择器能有效提升模型的回答决策质量，优于依赖模型自身置信度的校准过滤方法。但跨数据集（如NQ-Open）实现可靠的拒答能力仍是开放问题，强化学习直接优化模型自身的效果不稳定，未来需进一步探索泛化性更强的拒答机制。

Abstract: A useful language model should know when to answer and when to say "I don't know." Can we improve that decision by training the model itself or by training a separate component to check its answers? We study both approaches on Qwen3-14B for factual questions, using a score that rewards correct answers, penalizes mistakes, and allows abstention. Across three training seeds and two held-out confidence thresholds, a learned answer selector improves threshold-averaged utility over the original model's calibrated confidence filter. On TriviaQA, mean coverage increases from 20.2% to 30.9%, while the pooled error rate among scored answers decreases from 23.0% to 14.5% under exact-match grading. The tested reinforcement-learning changes do not establish consistent gains. On NQ-Open, the selector improves threshold-averaged utility over the filter but still performs worse than always abstaining. A partial human-reviewed audit preserves the TriviaQA finding. Our results support learning to select answers in this setting while showing that reliable abstention across datasets remains unresolved.

</details>


### [69] [Task Context Shapes Component Roles in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13895)
*Sovesh Mohapatra,Dani S Bassett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型中共享注意力头在不同任务中的任务依赖性行为，通过干预实验和线性响应映射，揭示任务上下文如何改变共享组件对图事实的响应及其对后续计算的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在不同任务中复用相同的注意力头，但仅识别共享头无法解释其在各任务中的不同功能。受大脑共享区域任务依赖性交互启发，采用网络视角分析模型计算，旨在理解共享参数如何支持不同计算。

Method: 在五个开源模型（四个家族，1.5-14B参数）中，使用相同的图事实（有向连接规范）比较固定模型组件跨任务的表现。测量干预对注意力头及后层激活状态的影响（影响力）及对答案的影响（重要性）。在Gemma-2-9B和Qwen3-14B中进行激活编辑，改变早期内部激活向量，切换模型读取的事实而指令固定。为每个提示构建两个线性响应映射（编辑前/后），从早期头的测量变化预测后期头的响应。用单独的事实干预验证映射的预测误差降低。

Result: 任务变化在五个模型中重新分配了影响力。激活编辑使相同注意力头响应不同的图事实，并以不同方式影响后续组件。对应条件的线性响应映射在单独事实干预中，相较于两映射平均，将均方预测误差在Gemma中降低53.6%，在Qwen中降低5.0%。

Conclusion: 任务上下文同时改变共享组件响应的图事实及其输出对后续计算的影响方式。网络层面的解释有助于理解模型如何利用共享参数执行不同计算。任务依赖性影响力的测量可指导旨在改变单一行为同时保留其他行为的干预。

Abstract: Large language models reuse the same attention heads across tasks, but identifying these shared heads does not explain what they do differently in each task. A natural source of inspiration is task-dependent interactions among shared brain regions, which motivate a network view of language-model computation. Here, we compare fixed model components across tasks using identical graph facts, statements specifying which directed connections are present. We measure how interventions affect attention heads and the activation state in later layers (influence), as well as the answer (importance). Task changes redistribute the influence in five open-weight models spanning four families and 1.5-14 billion parameters. In Gemma-2-9B and Qwen3-14B, an activation edit changes an early internal activation vector and switches which fact the model reads while the written instruction stays fixed. The same heads then respond to different graph facts and influence later components differently. For each prompt, we construct two linear response maps, one before the edit and one after it. Each map predicts a later head's response from a measured change in an earlier head. For a separate factual intervention, the map for the corresponding condition reduces mean squared prediction error by 53.6% in Gemma and 5.0% in Qwen compared with the average of the two maps. These findings show that task context changes both which graph facts the shared components respond to and how their outputs affect later computation. A network account may help explain how models use shared parameters to perform different computations. Measurements of task-dependent influence could also guide interventions intended to alter one behavior while preserving others.

</details>


### [70] [Structural Automata for Neural Semantic Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13896)
*Xiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究结构自动机在DRS解析三个阶段（解码、后处理、训练）的应用，并在AMR上评估解码。提出接受路径解码（APD），在剩余长度限制内维护已验证的完整路径，跟随模型首选token前提是该token可完成。APD保证输出被接受且无需回撤token。在紧长度限制下，APD在DRS上比局部掩码提升5.1和2.4 Smatch++点，在AMR上提升6.4和3.2点，与回撤方法相当。后处理显示无效输出集中于相同输入：有限训练数据下8个无约束样本中10.2%输入无有效输出，全量数据下仅0.4%，过滤效果接近APD（差0.2点）。训练阶段惩罚自动机拒绝token在前10%训练提升开发集Smatch++ 3.6-11.0点，但优势随交叉熵单独将拒绝token概率从14%降至2%以下而消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经语义解析器逐token生成意义表示，但良构性依赖于生成过程中展开的指代、作用域和图关系。约束解码虽防止局部无效token，但无法保证有效前缀能在固定输出长度限制内完成。需研究能保证全局有效性且不回撤的解码方法，并探索自动机在后处理和训练中的作用。

Method: 三阶段研究：1) 解码阶段：提出接受路径解码（APD），维护剩余长度内的已验证完整路径，仅当模型首选token也可完成时才采纳。2) 后处理阶段：评估无约束采样中无效输出的分布，对比过滤效果。3) 训练阶段：惩罚自动机拒绝的token，观察对早期训练的影响。在DRS和AMR任务上实验。

Result: APD在紧长度限制下显著优于局部掩码：DRS提升5.1/2.4 Smatch++点，AMR提升6.4/3.2点，与回撤方法相当但无需回撤。后处理：有限数据下10.2%输入无有效输出，全量数据仅0.4%，过滤效果差APD仅0.2点。训练：惩罚拒绝token前10%训练提升3.6-11.0点，但优势随交叉熵将拒绝token概率从14%降至2%以下而消失。
