Existing LLM-based tutoring systems use memory and retrieval to personalize responses, but the pedagogical rationale linking historical evidence to teaching strategies is not always explicit. We introduce RegulaTutor, a theory-informed framework for long-term personalized tutoring that connects historical evidence, learner interpretation, and learner-facing instruction. Guided by self-regulated learning (SRL) research, its tutoring harness operationalizes a cognitive, affective, metacognitive, and motivational (CAMM) framework for deriving evidence-supported learner interpretations and teaching policies. The resulting policies retain links to their supporting evidence and are updated as new observations arrive. This staged workflow also supports systematic refinement through PairRoute, an offline procedure that uses intermediate execution records to distinguish harness, model, and mixed failures. PairRoute proposes and evaluates targeted harness revisions, then constructs reviewed stage-level supervision for confirmed model failures remaining under the selected harness. We focus this refinement on historical evidence acquisition, which underpins personalized instruction and allows outputs to be checked against learning records and reference answers. RegulaTutor improves historical-evidence accuracy, learner diagnosis, and judged teaching quality on average across evaluated model configurations. PairRoute results show an endpoint improvement in standalone-model historical-evidence accuracy over the refinement trajectory. Together, these results highlight RegulaTutor's potential to support lifelong personalized education through instructional policies that evolve with learner histories and targeted improvements to the tutoring system.

</details>


### [138] [Local Diagnostics of Learned Event Representations for Anomaly Detection at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13437)
*Donatella Genovese,Philip Ploner,Simone Scardapane,Stefano Giagu,Thea Klaeboe Aarrestad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在COLLIDE-2V数据集(7.5亿事件,模拟高亮度LHC条件)上评估了监督和自监督神经嵌入策略在异常检测中的表现,使用自编码器在潜在空间的重构误差作为异常分数。监督目标(利用模拟过程标签)通常优于自监督方法,最佳配置下在所有保留过程中信号效率(10%背景接受率下)均有提升。论文还提出了本地诊断流程:在标准模型潜在空间拟合高斯混合模型,通过高层可观测量剖析各组件(无需过程标签),定位标记事件,按本地判别力排序可观测量。该排序恢复了保留过程的定义运动学,而异常分数与可观测量的相关性量化了分数跟踪它们的程度,揭示了最具判别力的可观测量并非分数最紧密跟随的那个情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测是LHC新物理搜索中最有前景的模型无关策略之一。在学习的低维表示中搜索稀有事件消除了预先承诺手工构造可观测量的需要,但牺牲了这些可观测量提供的即时物理解释。为了使异常检测进入发现流程,标记的事件必须在局部进行诊断,既要区分潜在新物理与建模错误或探测器效应,又要为针对性后续搜索识别可观测量。

Method: 在COLLIDE-2V数据集上评估监督和自监督神经嵌入策略,使用自编码器在潜在空间的重构误差作为异常分数。提出本地诊断流程:在标准模型潜在空间拟合高斯混合模型(GMM),通过高层可观测量剖析各组件(无需过程标签),定位标记事件,按本地判别力排序可观测量。同时计算异常分数与可观测量的相关性,量化分数跟踪可观测量的程度。

Result: 跨配置和基准信号,监督目标(利用模拟过程标签)通常优于自监督对应方法,最佳配置下在所有保留过程中将10%背景接受率下的信号效率提升,优于直接在运动学特征上训练的自编码器。本地诊断流程的排序恢复了保留过程的定义运动学;异常分数与可观测量的相关性揭示了最具判别力的可观测量并非分数最紧密跟随的那个情况。

Conclusion: 监督神经嵌入策略在LHC异常检测中表现优于自监督方法,结合本地诊断流程可有效识别新物理信号并提供物理可解释性。该方法为基于异常检测的发现流程提供了完整框架:从异常评分到本地化诊断再到可观测量识别,实现了模型无关新物理搜索与物理解释的统一。

Abstract: Anomaly detection, the identification of collision events that deviate from Standard Model expectations, is one of the most promising model-agnostic strategies for new-physics searches at the Large Hadron Collider. Searching for rare events in learned low-dimensional representations removes the need to commit to hand-crafted observables a priori, but sacrifices the immediate physical interpretation those observables provide. For anomaly detection to enter a discovery pipeline, flagged events must therefore be diagnosed locally, both to distinguish potential new physics from mismodeling or detector effects and to identify observables for targeted follow-up searches. We evaluate supervised and self-supervised neural embedding strategies on COLLIDE-2V, a 750-million-event dataset simulated under realistic High-Luminosity LHC conditions, using the reconstruction error of an autoencoder trained in the learned latent space as the anomaly score. Across the configurations and benchmark signals considered, supervised objectives, which exploit simulation process labels during training, generally outperform their self-supervised counterparts and, in their best configurations, improve on an autoencoder trained directly on kinematic features, raising the signal efficiency at 10% background acceptance on all held-out processes. We further introduce a local diagnostic pipeline that fits a Gaussian Mixture Model to the SM latent space, profiles each component through high-level observables without process labels, localizes the flagged events, and ranks observables by their local discriminating power. The ranking recovers the defining kinematics of the held-out processes, while correlating the anomaly score with the same observables quantifies how closely the score tracks them, revealing cases where the most discriminating observable is not the one the score follows most closely.

</details>


### [139] [Rollout-Time Intervention: Unlocking Agentic Reinforcement Learning Beyond Policy Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13438)
*Chen Xi,Ding Ruixue,Wang Zhigaoyuan,Zhang Fanrui,Zhang Qiang,Chen Boli,Wang Shihang,Wang Tianfu,Ren Ailin,Wang Pengyong,Xie Pengjun,Xiong Hui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Rollout-Time Intervention (RTI) 方法，通过在 rollout 阶段利用奖励侧证据（评估规则）将错误轨迹修正为成功轨迹，解决了基于策略的强化学习在任务超出模型能力边界时的探索稀疏和训练崩溃问题，并在五个基准上显著优于基线，使策略逼近甚至超越更强 LLM 的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的 LLM 代理训练受限于 on-policy 探索瓶颈：当任务难度超出当前策略能力时，有效轨迹极其稀疏，导致训练崩溃，无法突破能力边界。

Method: RTI 在 rollout 过程中引入奖励侧证据（评估规则），实时干预并修正错误轨迹为成功轨迹，生成高质量 off-policy 数据；再通过分布偏移过滤机制剔除偏移过大的轨迹，保证优化的稳定性与有效性。

Result: 在五个基准测试中，RTI 持续超越 RL 基线，后训练策略性能接近显著更强的当代 LLM，并成功突破原有能力边界。

Conclusion: RTI 通过 rollout 时干预与分布偏移过滤，有效缓解了 on-policy 探索瓶颈，为 LLM 代理在超难任务上的强化学习提供了一条可行路径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a key paradigm for improving large language model (LLM) agents, enabling stronger reasoning, planning, and tool-use in interactive environments. However, existing agent RL methods remain fundamentally constrained by an on-policy reasoning bottleneck: when tasks exceed the model's current capability boundary, effective trajectories become extremely sparse and training often collapses. We introduce Rollout-Time Intervention (RTI), a technique designed to elicit successful trajectories beyond the policy's intrinsic exploration capacity. RTI operates by leveraging reward-side evidence (i.e., evaluation rubrics) during rollout to convert erroneous trajectories into successful ones, providing effective learning signals for tasks that the policy rarely solves on its own. Although intervention trajectories are inherently off-policy, we find that they can effectively guide policy optimization when trajectories with excessive distributional shifts are filtered out. Extensive experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate that RTI consistently outperforms RL baselines, enabling the post-trained policy to approach the performance of substantially stronger contemporary LLMs and, more importantly, transcend its original capability boundary. The code is available at https://github.com/Alibaba-NLP/qqr.

</details>


### [140] [Agentic Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13455)
*Max van Duijn,Michiel van der Meer,Aske Plaat,Niki van Stein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文本为一本关于智能体LLM的书籍摘要，探讨了结合行动能力的大语言模型如何在现实世界中行动、交互及其对用户的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM虽具备强大的语言理解、推理和生成能力，且已影响人类决策，但缺乏直接在现实世界中行动和与其他智能体交互的能力，本书旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过系统性分析智能体LLM的工作原理、行动机制、多智能体交互模式，构建理论框架并探讨实际应用场景与用户影响。

Result: 提出智能体LLM将语言模型与现实行动能力相结合，实现自主决策、工具使用、环境交互及多智能体协作，显著扩展LLM的应用边界。

Conclusion: 智能体LLM代表AI发展的关键范式转变，从被动回答转向主动行动，将深刻重塑人机交互模式，但同时也带来安全性、可控性及伦理等新挑战。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have taken the world by storm. Their ability to communicate with us in our own language, and to reason about our world, has made them a favorite tool for writing, answering questions, and much more. Their answers already influence our decisions and our actions. What would happen if LLMs themselves could act in the world directly? What would happen if LLMs could interact with each other? Agentic LLMs combine the power of large language models with the ability to act in the real world. In this book we will discuss how agentic LLMs work, how they act, how they interact, and what this means for us, as users.

</details>


### [141] [SqueezeBench: Saturation-Resilient Evaluation of Mathematical Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13461)
*Pablo Manrique,Hai Xia,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SqueezeBench 是一个抗饱和的数学基准测试：每个问题固定一个整数函数 f(n)，仅知小 n 的值；基准维护上下界记录（可计算 Lean 函数及机器验证证明）。提交需证明新界限在所有 n 不劣于当前记录且在某些 n 严格更优，一旦通过双 Lean 内核验证即成为新记录。发布了 10 个极值组合问题及 20 个初始记录；前沿 LLM 代理在 1 小时/记录限制下获 82 次通过并改进所有记录；更新模型再获 60 次通过并改进 19/20 记录；8/10 问题仍至少有一个记录在渐近意义上落后于最佳已发表界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学基准要么因问题被解决而饱和，要么每个开放问题仅能被解决一次，难以持续衡量 AI 数学能力的进步。需要一种基准，使得每次成功都能提高门槛而非移除问题，从而实现长期、可量化的进度追踪。

Method: 每个问题固定一个整数函数 f(n)（仅知小 n 取值），维护下界与上界两条记录。记录是一个可计算 Lean 函数，附带机器验证证明其为 f 的界。提交需给出新函数并证明：(1) 它界住 f；(2) 在所有 n 上不劣于当前记录；(3) 在至少一个 n 上严格更优。两个独立 Lean 内核分别验证证明，维护者仅审阅报告排除大 n 处的穷举搜索。通过后新函数即刻成为下一轮必须超越的记录。

Result: 发布 10 个极值组合问题和 20 条初始记录。基于前沿 LLM 的代理（每记录 ≤1 小时）提交 82 次均被接受，改进了全部 20 条记录。随后两个更新模型以此为起点，再获 60 次接受并再次改进 19 条记录。8 个问题中至少有一条记录在渐近意义上仍落后于最佳已发表界限。

Conclusion: SqueezeBench 提供了一个可持续、抗饱和的数学基准，通过形式化验证实现无需人工裁判的证明检查，并能精确量化当前认证界限与真实函数值的差距。实验表明当前 AI 能在文献范围内持续改进记录，但在多数问题上仍未达到最优已知渐近界，显示出进一步提升的空间。

Abstract: AI systems are making rapid progress in mathematics, but this progress is hard to measure. Benchmarks of solved problems saturate, and in benchmarks of open problems each problem can be resolved for the first time only once. We present SqueezeBench, a benchmark that is resilient to saturation: a success raises the bar instead of removing the problem. Each problem fixes an integer function $f(n)$ whose values are known only for small $n$, and the benchmark keeps a lower and an upper record on it. A record is a computable Lean function with a machine-checked proof that it bounds $f$. A submission proposes a new function and proves that it bounds $f$, that it is no worse than the current record at any $n$, and that it is strictly better at some $n$. Once accepted, it becomes the record that the next submission has to beat. The system thus chooses the bound as well as its proof, and it may improve the record at a single $n$ or its growth rate. At every $n$, the gap between the two records shows how far the certified bounds are from determining $f(n)$. Every proof is checked by two independent Lean kernels, so no referee needs to check the mathematics. The checks also reject a function that computes $f$ by exhaustive search at small $n$, and a maintainer only reads the submitter's report and looks for such a search at larger $n$. We release ten problems from extremal combinatorics with twenty proved initial records and the admission tools. As a baseline, agents based on a frontier language model, with at most one hour per record, had 82 submissions accepted and improved every record, without going beyond the published literature. Agents based on two newer models then started from these records, had 60 more submissions accepted and improved 19 of the 20 records again. For eight of the ten problems, at least one record is still asymptotically behind the best published bound.

</details>


### [142] [A Local Agentic AI Framework to Generate Edge Case Scenarios for Safety Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13479)
*Victoria J. Hodge,Colin Paterson,Rob Alexander*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于笔记本电脑的本地代理AI框架，利用双LLM协作（生成+评判）和think-act-observe循环，自动生成自主系统的边缘场景，并产出可追溯的安全保证证据。该方法低成本、完全可控、保障数据隐私，解决了人工介入和规则方法在长尾场景多样性上的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统的安全保证需要生成边缘场景以识别危险故障、提升鲁棒性并验证操作设计域边界行为。现有方法多依赖人工介入或规则策略，限制了场景的多样性和对长尾分布的覆盖。亟需一种自动化、自适应、隐私安全且可追溯的边缘场景生成方案。

Method: 构建本地代理AI框架：1) 以系统需求、系统描述、环境描述为知识库注入上下文提示词；2) 主LLM通过think-act-observe-update-continue循环及轻量级JSON编码生成场景；3) 独立第二LLM按领域特定准则评判并细化场景；4) 仿真运行收集评价指标；5) 指标分析驱动开发（生成相似边缘场景）或探索（生成新颖场景）；6) 事后基于指标聚类与富文本主题分析，定位功能缺口及触发条件，形成可追溯的安全保证证据链。

Result: 验证了灵活自适应的本地代理AI框架在自动化边缘场景生成与产出可追溯安全证据方面的潜力。该方案成本低、完全可控、保障数据隐私安全，能有效覆盖长尾分布并生成多样化边缘场景。

Conclusion: 基于本地LLM的双模型协作代理框架，结合仿真反馈闭环与事后聚类分析，实现了低成本、隐私安全、可追溯的自动化边缘场景生成，为自主系统安全保证提供了可落地的工程化路径，克服了人工介入和规则方法的固有局限。

Abstract: Generating edge case scenarios is crucial for training, testing and safety assuring autonomous systems. This identifies hazardous failures to increase system reliability and robustness, ensures the system can still function safely on the boundaries of its operational design domain and learns the expected system behaviour for these edge cases. The rapid advancement in the generation and analysis capabilities of language models (LMs) has expanded their applicability and recent work has investigated LM scenario generation. However, many recent approaches rely on human-in-the-loop or rule-based strategies. This restricts the LM's ability to generate diverse and edge case scenarios; the long tail of the distribution. Our work uses local LMs in a laptop-based agentic AI framework for adaptive scenario generation. This is cheap, fully-controlled, ensures data privacy and security. It uses think, act, observe, update, and continue loops with lightweight JSON encoding to generate scenarios. We use the system requirements, system description and environment description as knowledge bases in contextual prompts for an LM to ensure scenario realism. A second, independent LM critiques each scenario using domain specific criteria so it can be refined. The refined scenarios are run in simulation and evaluation metrics collected. These metric scores are analysed and similar scenarios are created for edge cases (exploitation) or novel scenarios are generated to explore the scenario space. Post-hoc clustering using metric scores and thematic-type analysis of the rich-text scenario descriptions produced by the LM pinpoints functional gaps and their triggering conditions. This creates traceable evidence for the safety assurance case. Our work highlights the potential of a flexible and adaptive local agentic AI framework for automating edge case generation and producing traceable safety assurance evidence.

</details>


### [143] [Branching On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13481)
*Dongyang Chen,Shudong Liu,Shubo Li,Duoxun Tang,Jinwei Liang,Zehuan Wu,Lewei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 BranchOPD（分支式在线策略蒸馏），通过在学生不确定且与教师存在分歧的决策点分支重采样，利用共享前缀复用降低生成成本，并引入协议边界修复（PBR）缓解重复生成，在数学推理和智能体任务上显著超越标准 OPD 与多轨迹 OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线策略蒸馏（OPD）仅在单条学生轨迹上提供教师监督，每个决策点仅覆盖一种延续，导致在有限采样预算下监督覆盖面不足，且独立多轨迹采样存在大量冗余 token 开销。

Method: 1. 基于学生不确定性与教师-学生分歧选择信息量大的决策点分支；2. 从共享前缀重采样学生策略延续，构建主轨迹与分支轨迹并施加 token 级教师监督；3. 在数学任务以 token 级决策分支，在智能体任务以轮内决策分支；4. 引入协议边界修复（PBR），在协议约束下针对性监督以减少重复与无效生成。

Result: 在数学推理（avg@16 准确率提升 18.55 百分点）与 ALFWorld ID/OOD 成功率（提升 13.8 点）上全面超越 Vanilla OPD 与 7 轨迹 Multi-Rollout OPD；共享前缀复用使 ALFWorld 与 WebShop 的学生生成 token 成本分别降低约 19% 与 31%；消融实验证实在不确定决策点分支优于随机选择与轮首分支，能有效扩展后续行为覆盖。

Conclusion: BranchOPD 通过不确定性驱动的分支重采样与共享前缀复用，在保持计算效率的前提下大幅扩展了教师监督的决策覆盖面，结合 PBR 机制解决协议约束下的生成病态，为在线策略蒸馏提供了更高效且泛化性更强的范式。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides teacher supervision on student-generated trajectories, but a single trajectory realizes only one continuation at each decision, limiting supervision coverage under a finite sampling budget. We propose BranchOPD (Branching On-Policy Distillation), which selects informative decision points using student uncertainty and teacher-student disagreement, resamples student-policy continuations from shared prefixes, and applies token-level teacher supervision to both main trajectories and branches. BranchOPD operates at token-level decisions in mathematics and within-turn decisions in agent tasks. Shared-prefix reuse reduces student-generated token costs by approximately 19% on ALFWorld and 31% on WebShop relative to seven independent trajectories. We also introduce protocol-boundary repair (PBR), which uses targeted supervision under protocol constraints to mitigate repetition and wasted generation. BranchOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD and Multi-Rollout OPD (n=7) across all evaluated benchmarks, with gains over Vanilla OPD of 18.55 percentage points in mean mathematics accuracy over 16 sampled answers (avg@16) and 13.8 points in mean ALFWorld ID/OOD success. Mechanistic analyses and selection ablations show that branching at uncertain decisions expands subsequent behavior coverage and outperforms random selection and turn-initial branching.

</details>


### [144] [Humanize MLE Agents: Multi-Agent Session Alternation Beyond Single-Agent Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13494)
*Changye Li,Zijian Zhang,Muheng Li,Yiduo Pang,Tingjun Wu,Menghan Li,Yitong Liu,Hongkai Guo,Mengchi Chen,Chenyu He,Kaisheng Ma,Wei Xu,Song Han,Sihao Liu,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种交替双智能体工作流 HMA，通过会话级上下文重置解决长程MLE任务中的推理衰退和工具链过时问题，在MLE-bench上达成SOTA奖牌率78.2%，显著优于基线且泛化性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的MLE智能体依赖定制化工具链进行上下文工程，但随着单会话执行变长出现“长程减速”（推理强度下降、进度放缓）和“工具链过时”（底层模型升级导致收益消失）问题，动机是设计跨会话协作的工作流级方法。

Method: 将交替智能体会话建模为上下文重置半马尔可夫决策过程（CR-SMDP），给出交替策略超越单智能体的充分条件、实验预算界限及会话上限保持优势的条件；据此设计HMA：双智能体共享工作空间，每会话最多k次实验，会话间重置上下文，无需额外任务专用工具链或先验知识。

Result: 在75个Kaggle任务（MLE-bench）上，HMA任意奖牌率达78.2%，比最强开源基线高8.0个百分点，且仅用其1/4时间预算；跨5对异构智能体的6种配置平均提升6.4点；内核优化案例达878轮，距社区榜首仅1.6%。

Conclusion: 工作流级交替配合会话级上下文重置能有效缓解长程推理衰退与工具链过时，HMA以轻量级、免工具链设计实现跨任务、跨智能体对的稳健增益，展示了通用的长程MLE代理新范式。

Abstract: Existing LLM-based agent approaches for long-horizon machine learning engineering (MLE) often use context engineering, implemented through custom harnesses that manage tools, skills, and accumulated experience. However, we find that as single-session execution lengthens, the agent's reasoning intensity declines alongside slowing progress, summarized as long-horizon deceleration, and the benefits of these custom harnesses vanish as the underlying model improves, summarized as harness staleness. These findings suggest that their benefits may not persist across versions, which motivates a workflow-level approach in which agents build on one another's work across sessions. We model these alternating agent sessions as a context-reset semi-Markov decision process (CR-SMDP) and establish sufficient conditions for alternation to exceed single-agent reference limits, derive an explicit experiment-budget bound, and characterize when session caps preserve this advantage. Guided by this analysis, we introduce Humanize MLE Agents (HMA), which alternates two agents on a shared workspace, limits each session to at most k experiments and resets the context between sessions, without additional task-specific harness or prior knowledge supplements. Across 75 Kaggle tasks in MLE-bench, HMA reaches a state-of-the-art any-medal rate of 78.2%, exceeding the strongest open-source baseline by 8.0 points with a quarter of its time budget. Moreover, among six configurations spanning five heterogeneous agent pairs, HMA improves any-medal rate over the constituent baselines by 6.4 points on average, demonstrating cross-pair generality. A supplementary case on kernel optimization reaches 878 cycles, within 1.6% of the community leaderboard leader, demonstrating applicability beyond MLE.

</details>


### [145] [Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Language Model with Heterogeneous Evidence Integration for Clinical Prognosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13510)
*Hamza Shafiq,Jialu Tang,Ting Dang,Jun Hu,Aaqib Saeed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM，一种多模态语言模型，用于临床预后预测。PRISM结合逐特征数值分词、时序心电图的近期加权聚合、双模态人群记忆库，并在表示空间检索相似训练队列就诊的均值池化嵌入。在MDS-ED基准（1443任务）上，PRISM在诊断、恶化、ICU入住、死亡率预测中AUROC分别达86.2%、93.1%、91.8%、92.8%，使用96小时院前窗口。同窗口输入下PRISM达0.8895 AUROC，优于同输入紧凑融合模型（0.7652）。消融显示数值编码、院前历史、检索均显著提升性能。外部MC-MED队列微调后AUROC 0.8611，优于本地梯度提升（0.8227）和仅源模型（0.7841）。结果支持结构感知多模态融合与监督外部适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预后需要整合结构化测量、生理波形、纵向病史和人群背景。现有方法难以有效融合这些异构模态，且缺乏利用人群级相似病例的检索机制。

Method: PRISM采用逐特征数值分词编码EHR结构化数据，用近期加权聚合处理纵向心电图波形，构建双模态（EHR+波形）人群记忆库。检索在表示空间进行：模型接收相似训练队列就诊的均值池化嵌入，而非将单个检索记录放入提示。

Result: MDS-ED基准1443任务：诊断AUROC 86.2%，恶化93.1%，ICU入住91.8%，死亡率92.8%（使用96小时院前窗口）。同窗口输入下PRISM 0.8895 AUROC，优于UniPACT 0.8937和MDS-ED 0.8889。同输入紧凑融合模型仅0.7652 vs PRISM 0.9092。消融：文本序列化替代数值编码降3.66点，移除院前历史降1.97点，零检索降0.99点。真实检索比随机邻居高0.97宏平均点（p=0.067）。外部MC-MED微调达0.8611 AUROC，优于本地梯度提升0.8227和仅源模型0.7841。

Conclusion: 结构感知的多模态整合（数值分词、波形聚合）与表示空间人群检索显著提升临床预后预测。监督外部微调能有效适配新队列。

Abstract: Clinical prognosis requires integrating structured measurements, physiological waveforms, longitudinal history, and population context. We introduce PRISM (Population-Retrieved Inference for Structured Multimodal Prognosis), a multimodal language model that combines per-feature numerical tokenization, recency-weighted aggregation of longitudinal ECGs, and a dual-modality population memory bank. Retrieval operates in representation space: the model receives mean-pooled embeddings of similar training-cohort encounters, while no individual retrieved record enters the prompt. On the 1,443-task MDS-ED benchmark, PRISM achieves AUROCs of 86.2% for diagnosis, 93.1% for deterioration, 91.8% for ICU admission, and 92.8% for mortality. These results use a 96-hour pre-arrival window unavailable to reported baselines. With benchmark-window inputs only, PRISM reaches 0.8895 AUROC, compared with 0.8937 for UniPACT and 0.8889 for MDS-ED. A matched-input compact fusion model using the same five evidence streams and frozen encoders reaches 0.7652 AUROC, versus 0.9092 for PRISM. Replacing numerical EHR encoding with text serialization reduces AUROC by 3.66 points; removing pre-arrival history reduces it by 1.97 points; and zeroing retrieval reduces it by 0.99 points. Genuine retrieval outperforms random-neighbor retrieval by 0.97 macro-average points, although a paired task-level sign test does not establish a uniform advantage (p=0.067). On the external MC-MED cohort, supervised fine-tuning reaches 0.8611 AUROC, compared with 0.8227 for locally trained gradient boosting and 0.7841 for source-only PRISM. These results support structure-aware multimodal integration and supervised external adaptation.

</details>


### [146] [Causal Effect Identifiability in Causal Abstractions: A Strict Hierarchy of Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13520)
*Clément Yvernes,Marianne Clausel,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究因果抽象框架下的因果效应可识别性，形式化了多种可识别性概念并建立层级结构，证明了因果效应可在图集合上被识别而不需要单一的统一因果推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推断方法通常要求完全指定的因果图，这在复杂或高维场景中往往不切实际。因果抽象通过保留部分因果信息定义兼容因果图的等价类，提供了更灵活的框架，但现有文献对图集合层面的可识别性概念存在模糊和空白。

Method: 在因果抽象框架下，直接在兼容因果图的集合（而非单个因果图）层面形式化因果效应可识别性，定义并组织多种可识别性概念为层级结构，分析它们之间的关系与差异。

Result: 建立了因果效应可识别性概念的层级体系；证明了因果效应可以在图集合上被识别，但不存在在所有兼容图上统一成立的单一因果推导；揭示了现有文献中的概念空白和歧义。

Conclusion: 因果抽象框架下的可识别性研究需要区分不同层级的识别概念，图集合层面的识别不等价于统一识别，这为因果推断在部分因果知识下的理论基础提供了更细致的理解。

Abstract: Identifying causal effects from observational data typically requires a fully specified causal graph, an assumption that is often unrealistic in complex or high-dimensional settings. Causal abstractions address this limitation by retaining only partial causal information, thereby defining equivalence classes of compatible causal graphs. In this paper, we study causal effect identifiability directly at the level of these graph collections, rather than at the level of individual causal diagrams. We formalize several notions of identifiability in this setting and organize them into a hierarchy that clarifies their relationships and differences. In particular, we establish that a causal effect may be identified over a collection of graphs without admitting a single causal derivation that holds uniformly across all compatible graphs. These results help reveal gaps and clarify ambiguities in the existing literature.

</details>


### [147] [Beyond Domain-Routed Supervision: Cross-Teacher Guarding for Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13521)
*Shi-Yu Tian,Yong Liu,Ximan Liu,Tianrui Zhu,Sule Bai,Xinwei Yang,Ruiqi Liu,Kun-Yang Yu,Ming Yang,Zhi Zhou,Yu-Feng Li,Yipeng Sun,Gu Simiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Cross-Teacher Guarding for MOPD (Road MOPD)，通过引入非路由教师作为跨域辅助评论员，利用教师一致性进行 token 级信用分配和梯度投影，缓解多教师在线蒸馏中的梯度冲突，显著提升劣势领域性能并在高冲突场景下获得 5 个百分点以上增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师在线蒸馏 (MOPD) 虽通过领域路由将特定数据分配给专业教师，但所有领域的更新仍作用于共享参数。当教师在响应风格和领域专长上差异较大时，单独有效的监督信号在联合优化中会产生持续的梯度冲突，导致能力退化。

Method: Road MOPD 引入非路由教师作为跨域辅助评论员，在相同学生轨迹上提供监督。核心包含两部分：1) 跨教师引导的信用分配，利用教师一致性增强共同支持的 token 级修正并衰减冲突信号；2) 预防性多教师梯度投影，利用同一轨迹的辅助梯度约束潜在冲突更新，并自适应控制投影强度以保留目标领域学习信号。

Result: 实验表明，Road MOPD 有效缓解了劣势领域的性能退化，在高冲突设置下性能提升超过 5 个百分点，同时在低冲突和三领域设置下提升整体性能。

Conclusion: 通过跨教师协作的信用分配与梯度投影机制，Road MOPD 成功解决了多教师在线蒸馏中的梯度冲突问题，实现了跨领域知识的有效整合，为多教师蒸馏提供了更鲁棒的优化框架。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) extends on-policy distillation (OPD) by routing domain-specific data to specialized teachers, integrating their capabilities through dense token-level supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, domain routing only distinguishes the supervision sources for different domains; updates from all domains still act on shared parameters. When teachers differ substantially in response style and domain expertise, supervision that is effective in isolation may still lead to persistent gradient conflicts and capability degradation during joint optimization. To address this challenge, we propose Cross-Teacher Guarding for MOPD (Road MOPD), which introduces non-routed teachers as auxiliary cross-domain critics on the same student trajectories and coordinates multi-teacher supervision through token-level credit assignment and gradient updates. Specifically, cross-teacher-guided credit assignment uses teacher agreement to strengthen token-level corrections jointly supported by the teachers and attenuate conflicting signals; preventive multi-teacher gradient projection uses auxiliary gradients from the same trajectories to constrain potentially conflicting updates and adaptively controls projection strength to retain target-domain learning signals. In experiments, Road MOPD mitigates degradation in the disadvantaged domain, gains over 5 percentage points under high conflict, and improves overall performance in low-conflict and three-domain settings.

</details>


### [148] [MAGMA-Bench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Embodied Agents under Dynamic Tasks and Execution Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13534)
*Loan Bernat,Matthieu Grard,Ariane Herbulot,Florent Lamiraux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGMA-Bench 是一个架构无关的基准测试，用于评估动态多机器人环境中长时程任务推理能力，揭示了当前模型在复杂、多机器人、在线扰动场景下的显著不足，并提供了大规模训练数据与专家轨迹生成管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么关注动态任务执行，要么关注多智能体协调，缺乏对二者交叉的系统评估；长时程具身智能体需在异构机器人协作中维持任务相关信息并自适应决策，亟需统一评测平台。

Method: 构建 MAGMA-Bench，采用结构化技能级接口将任务级推理与感知、底层控制解耦；设计逐渐增加的任务时程与可控在线扰动（执行失败、任务中断、任务更新），要求异构能力机器人协作；评测本地可部署的开放权重规划器、多智能体状态追踪策略及前沿大模型。

Result: 即使最强模型在无扰动回合中仍大量失败，引入在线扰动后成功率进一步下降；监督微调显著提升小模型性能，但在长时程和多机器人任务上仍大幅落后；发布基准、261,646 条任务级训练样本及架构无关的专家轨迹生成管线。

Conclusion: MAGMA-Bench 填补了动态任务执行与多智能体协调交叉评测的空白，量化揭示了当前模型在持久任务推理中的局限，为后续长时程、异构多机器人具身智能研究提供了标准化评测与大规模训练资源。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied agents must maintain task-relevant information, coordinate heterogeneous robots, and adapt their decisions as execution unfolds. Existing benchmarks investigate dynamic task execution and embodied multi-agent coordination, but provide limited evaluation of their intersection. We introduce MAGMA-Bench, an architecture-agnostic benchmark for persistent task reasoning in dynamic multi-robot environments. MAGMA-Bench evaluates agents across increasing task horizons and controlled online perturbations, including execution failures, task interruptions, and mission updates, with scenarios requiring coordination across robots with heterogeneous capabilities. It uses a structured skill-level interface to separate task-level reasoning from perception and low-level control. We evaluate locally deployable open-weight planners, multi-agent state-tracking strategies, and frontier models. Even the strongest evaluated models fail on a substantial fraction of clean episodes, and their success rates decrease further under online perturbations. Supervised adaptation greatly improves a small model, but performance remains substantially lower on longer and multi-robot missions. We release the benchmark, 261,646 task-level training examples, and an architecture-agnostic expert-trace generation pipeline.

</details>


### [149] [Constructing Pareto Frontiers of Optimal Reasoning Termination Methods for Overthinking in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13570)
*Weixin Guan,Liang Li,Shiyu Xie,Jiapeng Liu,Bing Li,Peng Fu,Chengyang Fang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Can Ma,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究推理模型在测试时扩展中的过度思考现象，提出Step-wise Truncation Probing (STP)量化过度思考严重程度，构建统一框架评估终止策略的理论缓解潜力，并发布OTBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型通过测试时扩展可提升性能，但持续推理可能变得无效甚至有害（过度思考）。现有评估仅对比准确率和token使用量，缺乏过度思考严重程度的参考基准及终止策略理论缓解潜力的量化分析。

Method: 1. 提出Step-wise Truncation Probing (STP)：在固定采样推理轨迹上保留多个前缀并生成答案，以最早达到最大实证准确率的位置为参考，定义Overthinking Ratio（后续生成思维token比例）和Accuracy Degradation（从参考点到完整思考的准确率下降）。2. 将动态路由、固定预算、动态早退纳入统一框架，量化不同终止策略的理论缓解潜力，构建token-accuracy Pareto前沿。3. 基于STP构建可复用基准OTBench，覆盖六模型四基准组，产出70.5B推理token及探测测量。

Result: 通过STP量化了模型-任务层面的过度思考程度，统一框架下对比了终止策略的理论最优表现，OTBench提供大规模可复用的探测数据支持后续研究。

Conclusion: STP及统一框架为过度思考提供了标准化度量与理论分析工具，OTBench作为基准资源将推动测试时扩展中终止策略的系统性研究与改进。

Abstract: Reasoning models can benefit from test-time scaling, but continued reasoning may become ineffective or harmful, a phenomenon termed overthinking. Evaluations against full thinking compare accuracy and token usage but lack references for overthinking severity and strategies' theoretical mitigation potential. To characterize overthinking at the model-task level, we first introduce Step-wise Truncation Probing (STP), repeatedly generating answers from multiple retained prefixes along each fixed sampled reasoning trajectory. Using each trajectory's earliest measured position attaining maximum empirical accuracy as a reference, we define Overthinking Ratio and Accuracy Degradation as the fraction of thinking tokens generated afterward and the accuracy decrease from this reference to full thinking, respectively. Secondly, we then formulate dynamic routing, fixed budgets, and dynamic early exit within a unified framework to quantify the theoretical mitigation potential of different termination strategies. Based on this unified framework, we use the complete STP measurements to construct a token-accuracy Pareto frontier for each strategy under a common normalized thinking-token budget. We further develop OTBench, a reusable benchmark built from the STP process, which produced 70.5B reasoning tokens and the corresponding probing measurements across six models and four benchmark groups.

</details>


### [150] [MENTOR: Learning from Failure with On-Policy Pedagogical Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13594)
*Guanlan Yue,Hang Yu,Xiaoyong Huang,Ruipeng Li,Wei Zhang,Bingchang Liu,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对RLVR中的零奖励崩溃问题，提出MENTOR多智能体框架，实时诊断失败并生成教学提示，作为即插即用模块集成到GRPO等训练流程，有效缓解崩溃并显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR训练易遭遇零奖励崩溃，即任务难度突然增加导致学习信号消失。传统方法丢弃失败轨迹浪费了诊断模型认知缺口的宝贵信息；现有解决方案多为静态离线干预（如预定义提示库）或事后修正，缺乏自适应、前瞻性的指导以逃离崩溃。

Method: 提出MENTOR，一个多智能体框架，作为实时导师。它在线诊断失败根因，动态生成针对性教学提示引导学习智能体下一次尝试。MENTOR作为即插即用模块，无缝集成到现有策略优化流水线（如GRPO），无需修改训练基础设施。

Result: 在数学和代码生成任务上进行大量实验，MENTOR有效缓解零奖励崩溃，超越最先进基线。在Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct上实现高达78.3%的平均相对增益。

Conclusion: MENTOR通过在线教学干预范式转变，利用失败轨迹中的诊断信息实现自适应恢复，为RLVR训练提供了实用且高效的解决方案，显著提升大规模推理模型的训练稳定性和性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is a key driver for Large Reasoning Models, but it is vulnerable to "zero-reward collapse", a training failure where a sudden increase in task difficulty causes the learning signal to vanish. We argue that the conventional approach of discarding these failed trajectories is wasteful, as they contain valuable diagnostic information about the model's cognitive gaps, a bright side that can be leveraged for recovery. Existing solutions, however, often regress to static, "off-policy-style" interventions like pre-defined hint libraries or perform post-hoc refinement, failing to provide the adaptive, forward-looking guidance needed to escape the collapse. To address this, we propose a paradigm shift towards on-policy pedagogical intervention and introduce MENTOR, a multi-agent framework that acts as a live mentor. MENTOR diagnoses the root cause of failure in real-time and dynamically generates targeted, instructional prompts to guide the learning agent's next attempt. As a plug-and-play module, MENTOR integrates seamlessly into existing policy optimization pipelines like GRPO without modifying the training infrastructure. Extensive experiments on math and code generation show that MENTOR effectively mitigates zero-reward collapse, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. Notably, it achieves massive average relative gains of up to 78.3% on Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct over its base model.

</details>


### [151] [Humanize Olympiad Agents: A Multi-Session Workflow for Olympiad Problem Solving with Solve, Review, Wiki, Verify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13632)
*Zhengyang Zhang,Jing Xiong,Hongzhou Lin,Juihui Chung,Zijian Zhang,Song Han,Sihao Liu,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HOA（Humanize Olympiads Agent），一个开源的流程级框架，通过三个互补反馈通道（独立审查员、结构化维基、Lean 验证器）协调多智能体会话，显著提升 LLM 在长时程科学与奥林匹克任务上的表现。实验表明，搭配 GPT-5.6 Sol 时 HOA 在 IMO、IPhO、IOI 2026 满分，PutnamBench 100%，Lean-Eval 排名第一；搭配开源模型 Kimi-K3 时亦达金牌水平，缩小了开闭源模型的能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体在长时程任务上仍存在脆弱性：早期错误传播、领域知识缺失导致停滞、表面成功可能掩盖任务误解或弱化。且当前先进系统（如 Lean 形式化 Navier-Stokes 求解、Erdős 猜想自动求解）均未同时开放底层模型与完整推理栈，缺乏可复现、系统化的反馈机制。

Method: HOA 引入三大反馈通道协调多智能体会话：1) 独立审查员对每个候选解进行评估并给出可执行反馈；2) 结构化维基提供预训练中可能缺失或不可靠的任务特定知识；3) 验证器将生成的 Lean 证明与可信挑战陈述核对。框架为开源、流程级设计，支持闭源与开源基座模型。

Result: GPT-5.6 Sol + HOA：IMO、IPhO、IOI 2026 满分；IChO 2026 接近满分；PutnamBench 672 道全解（100%）；Lean-Eval 解决 251/304（82.6%），排名第一。Kimi-K3 + HOA：IMO、IPhO、IOI 满分；IChO 97.1%（金牌级）；在 IMO 与 IPhO 接近闭源 GPT-5.6 Sol + /Goal 基线，在 IChO 与 IOI 超越之，证明系统化反馈能弥合基座模型能力差距。

Conclusion: HOA 通过结构化多通道反馈机制，有效解决了长时程科学推理中的错误传播、知识缺失与任务弱化问题。该框架在开闭源模型上均达最先进水平，并展示了系统化反馈可显著缩小模型能力鸿沟，为构建可靠、可复现的科学智能体提供了开放基础设施。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are rapidly improving on long-horizon scientific and olympiad tasks. Recent systems have produced a Lean-formalized resolution of the Navier--Stokes problem and autonomous solutions to four open questions in the Erdős Conjectures database. Yet neither system releases both its underlying model and complete inference stack. More broadly, a single agent session remains brittle on difficult problems: early errors can propagate, missing domain knowledge can stall progress, and apparent success can mask a misinterpretation or weakening of the original task. We introduce HOA (Humanize Olympiads Agent), an open, flow-level framework that coordinates multiple agent sessions through three complementary feedback channels. First, an independent reviewer evaluates each candidate and provides actionable feedback. Second, a structured wiki supplies task-specific knowledge that may be missing or unreliable in model pretraining. Third, a verifier checks generated Lean proofs against trusted challenge statements. With GPT-5.6 Sol, HOA achieves perfect scores on the IMO, IPhO, and IOI 2026, near-full marks on the IChO 2026 problem set, and solves all 672 problems (100%) on PutnamBench. On the Lean-Eval leaderboard, HOA ranks first, solving 251 of 304 problems (82.6%). We further evaluate HOA with open models. Using Kimi-K3, HOA achieves full scores on the IMO, IPhO, and IOI, and a gold-level score of 97.1% on the IChO. These results closely match the closed-source GPT-5.6 Sol + /Goal baseline on the IMO and IPhO, while exceeding it on the IChO and IOI, demonstrating that systematic feedback can help bridge substantial differences in base-model capability.

</details>


### [152] [Learning Size Independent Constraint Models from Input Data and Positive and Negative Example Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13640)
*Helmut Simonis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种约束获取工具，能从输入数据和正负例解中自动生成与规模无关的约束程序，输出MiniZinc代码和文本描述，在PTHG21挑战题上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决从实例解自动学习可泛化到未见规模的约束模型，降低建模门槛，实现约束编程的自动化获取。

Method: 系统首先基于全局约束和二元约束的典型模式寻找特定规模的模型，然后将这些模型泛化为规模无关的模型，能在未见输入数据上运行，最终输出MiniZinc代码和文本描述。

Result: 在PTHG21挑战的示例问题上评估，展示了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工具成功实现了从正负例中自动获取可泛化的约束模型，为约束编程的自动化建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: In this paper we describe a Constraint Acquisition Tool that generates size independent constraint programs from input data and positive and negative example solutions of multiple problem instances. The system finds size specific models based on typical constraint patterns over global and binary constraints, then generalizes the models to a size independent model that can be run on unseen input data. The system produces MiniZinc code as well as a textual description of the model. We evaluate the tool on the example problems of the PTHG21 Challenge and show the feasibility of the approach.

</details>


### [153] [A Multi-Agent LLM System for Automated Tuning of Specialized Neural Network Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13663)
*Ivan Moloshnikov,Dmitry Kunitsyn,Alexander Sboev,Maksim Skorokhodov,Roman Rybka,Yuanchao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体大语言模型框架，用于自动调优机器学习模型中的关键组件。框架包含分析师、编码员、记录员三个角色，在沙箱环境中仅生成指定组件的代码，保持整体网络结构不变。在脉冲神经网络分类和物理信息神经网络求解高阶微分方程两类任务上验证，显著优于基线：分类准确率提升13和6个百分点，误差降低数百至数千倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法通常为两级：专家选择方法类别，类内关键组件选择关键且多为手动。需解决这些组件的自动调优，并提出可跨不同性质模型迁移的通用框架。

Method: 构建三角色多智能体LLM框架（分析师、编码员、记录员），配备实验日志和沙箱执行环境。框架在开放但语义狭窄的空间操作：智能体仅为指定组件生成代码，整体网络结构保持不变。

Result: 在五个任务上验证：MNIST和Digits脉冲分类准确率分别高出13和6个百分点；广义非线性薛定谔方程（GNLSE）单孤子解解析解误差降低约2491倍；不变量保持误差在选定高斯扰动运行中降低约325倍，重复初始化平均降低超100倍；Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程质量漂移降低14倍。

Conclusion: 该框架能在不改变内部结构的情况下，自动调优不同性质任务的关键组件，展现出跨模型迁移的通用调优能力。

Abstract: Many machine learning approaches are two-level: an expert selects a class of methods, within this class the choice of key components is critical and usually manual. This paper addresses the automatic tuning of these components and proposes a framework transferable across models of different nature. The multi-agent large language model (LLM) framework has three roles---analyst, coder, and recorder~---with an experiment log and a sandboxed execution environment. Unlike systems that optimize the entire ML script or search architectures in a discrete space, the framework operates over an open but semantically narrow space: the agent generates code only for the specified components, leaving the overall network structure unchanged. The approach is validated in two directions: tuning a classification spiking neural network with correlation learning, and solving high-order differential equations with Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINN). Across five tasks---spiking classification on MNIST and Digits, and boundary-value problems for the generalized nonlinear Schrödinger equation (GNLSE) and Kuramoto--Sivashinsky (KS) equations---the agent outperforms the baselines: classification accuracy is 13 percentage points (MNIST) and 6 percentage points (Digits) higher; the error against the analytical single-soliton solution is reduced approximately 2491-fold; the invariant-preservation error drops approximately 325-fold in the selected GNLSE run with a Gaussian perturbation and by more than 100-fold on average over repeated initializations; and the mass drift drops 14-fold for the KS problem with a Gaussian perturbation. The framework thus tunes key components on tasks of different nature without changing its internal structure.

</details>


### [154] [A Step Harder, Not a Step Too Far: The Benefits and Limits of Curriculum Difficulty Progression in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13669)
*Ronghan Li,Fei Chen,Mingyu Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRPO中基于课程学习的分阶段难度推进策略优于固定中等难度选择，但收益呈非单调性，适度推进至更难样本提升性能，过度推进反而下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO中中等难度样本能提供信息量大的群体相对学习信号，但固定难度估计随策略演变会失效，且持续选择中等难度样本是否能达到最优最终性能尚不清楚，需要探索分阶段难度推进是否能进一步提升性能。

Method: 重新估计当前策略下的样本难度，设计分阶段课程学习策略，逐步将活跃难度带向更难样本方向推进，在GSM8K数据集上对比固定中等难度课程与不同阶段推进策略的效果。

Result: 实验显示固定中等难度课程测试准确率为0.6449±0.0024，推进至中等偏难难度带提升至0.6591±0.0077，但进一步推进至更难难度带下降至0.6405±0.0053，表明分阶段推进优于固定选择但收益非单调。

Conclusion: 有效的GRPO课程学习应适度推进至中等难度之外，同时避免过度推进至过难样本，需平衡难度推进程度以最大化最终模型性能。

Abstract: Curriculum learning typically organizes training samples from easy to hard. In GRPO, medium-difficulty samples are attractive for online selection because they are more likely to yield informative group-relative learning signals, and prioritizing them can improve training efficiency. However, it remains unclear whether persistently selecting such samples also leads to the strongest final model performance, or whether training can benefit from progressing toward harder samples. Meanwhile, sample difficulty changes as the policy evolves, so fixed difficulty estimates can become misaligned with the current policy. We therefore re-estimate sample difficulty under the current policy and, inspired by the easy-to-hard principle of curriculum learning, design and evaluate a staged curriculum that progressively shifts the active difficulty band toward harder samples. Experiments on GSM8K show that persistent medium-difficulty selection does not achieve the strongest observed performance. Across three random seeds, progressing to a moderately harder difficulty band improves test accuracy to $0.6591 \pm 0.0077$, compared with $0.6449 \pm 0.0024$ for the fixed-medium curriculum. However, further progression to a harder difficulty band reduces accuracy to $0.6405 \pm 0.0053$. These results show that staged difficulty progression can outperform persistent medium-difficulty selection, but its benefit is non-monotonic. Effective GRPO curricula can therefore benefit from progressing beyond medium difficulty, while avoiding excessive progression toward overly difficult samples.}

</details>


### [155] [A Baseline Assessment for the Agentic Web Technology Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13703)
*Vagner Figueredo de Santana,Diogo Cortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MASTIF（多智能体系统测试框架），对 3 个 LLM、3 个协议、4 个智能体实现框架在 300 个 Mind2Web 基准任务上进行基线评估，结果表明针对特定任务选择技术栈可显著提升任务完成率、效率分、社会价值稳定性分，并降低冗余度与完成时间，为多智能体系统的设计、评估与开发提供了度量基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驱动的智能体协议、框架与技术层出不穷，导致开发者在技能、治理、基础设施等方面面临挑战，缺乏系统性评估不同组件协同效果的方法，难以在 Agentic Web 生态中做出最优技术选型。

Method: 构建 MASTIF 评估框架，选取 3 个 LLM、3 个通信协议、4 个智能体实现框架组合成多种技术栈，在 300 个 Mind2Web 任务上运行实验，采集任务完成率、效率分、社会价值稳定性分、冗余度、完成时间等指标，对比不同技术栈的表现。

Result: 针对特定任务选择技术栈可使任务完成率、效率分、社会价值稳定性分显著提升，同时降低输出冗余度与任务完成时间；不同组件组合对各指标影响程度不同，揭示了最优组合模式。

Conclusion: MASTIF 为 Agentic Web 技术栈提供了首个系统性基线评估，证实了任务感知的技术选型优势，为后续多智能体系统的设计、基准测试与演进提供了可复用的度量体系与实证参考。

Abstract: The fast pace in which protocols, frameworks, and technologies are being proposed for LLM-based agents results in a complex scenario for anyone aiming to create state-of-the-art AI solutions. Without an assessment on how different protocols, frameworks, and libraries work together to compose an Agentic Web solution, developers and organizations face different challenges (e.g., skilling, governance, infrastructure) even before becoming part of the Agentic Web ecosystem. In this context, we propose a system for performing Agentic Web evaluations called MASTIF (Multi-Agent System TestIng Framework) and present a baseline assessment of Agentic Web technologies, including 3 LLMs, 3 protocols, and 4 agent implementation frameworks, against 300 Mind2Web benchmark tasks. Results suggest that selecting a task-specific technology stack may result in gains of task completion rate, efficiency score, and social value stability scores; and reductions in verbosity and time to complete tasks. With the presented results, we expect to present a baseline in terms of metrics and results for this complex and fast evolving scenario for those aiming at designing, assessing, and developing multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [156] [Adaptive DHiSparse: Speculative Decoding over a Load-Adaptive KV Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13708)
*Naji Alazhar,Wassel Alazhar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Adaptive DHiSparse，一种支持分层稀疏 KV 缓存上的投机解码并能根据负载自适应调整驻留策略的系统。它在保护必要工作集的同时，利用空闲 GPU 内存保留更多历史，并通过精确融合检索恢复其余部分。实验表明，在相同延迟约束下，该方法显著提升吞吐并发和输出速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文服务受限于 KV 缓存内存，硬件算力利用率低。现有 HiSparse 虽将历史放入主机内存并按需获取，但采用固定缓存预算，且不支持 DSpark、DFlash2 等草稿模型投机解码。

Method: Adaptive DHiSparse：在分层稀疏 KV 上支持投机解码，动态调整驻留策略。保护必需工作集，GPU 内存富余时保留额外历史，其余通过精确融合检索恢复。DSpark 和 DFlash2 保持原有提议/接受规则：查询共享缺失行检索，草稿 KV 使用窗口界定环形缓冲，验证分阶段在层组间复用。

Result: 在 100K 上下文、并发 128 下，GLM-5.3 配合 DFlash2 达到 793 output tokens/s/解码 GPU，比 HiSparse 高 1.45 倍，比非投机自适应服务高 1.27 倍。在相同延迟界限下，并发提升达 4 倍，输出提升 2.3–3.7 倍。DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 500K 上下文、并发 512 时，配合 DSpark 输出为常驻服务的 1.85 倍。负载突变时，可选驻留将主机获取从 14.2% 降至 1.4%，并在压力下释放内存。

Conclusion: Adaptive DHiSparse 通过自适应驻留与投机解码协同，显著提升长上下文服务的吞吐与并发能力，同时在负载变化时动态平衡主机获取开销与 GPU 内存占用，实现了更高的硬件利用率。

Abstract: Long-context serving runs out of KV memory before the hardware reaches its useful throughput. HiSparse stores history in host memory and fetches only the selected entries, but it uses one cache budget at every load and does not handle draft-model speculative decoding such as DSpark and DFlash2. We present Adaptive DHiSparse, which supports speculative decoding over hierarchical sparse KV and adapts residency to load. It protects the required working set, retains additional history while GPU memory is spare, and recovers the rest through exact fused retrieval. DSpark and DFlash2 keep their proposal and acceptance rules: queries share missing-row retrieval, draft KV uses a window-bounded ring, and verification staging is reused across layer groups. At 100K context and concurrency 128, GLM-5.3 with DFlash2 delivers 793 output tokens/s per decode GPU, 1.45$\times$ HiSparse and 1.27$\times$ non-speculative adaptive serving. Held to the same latency bounds as HiSparse, it sustains up to 4$\times$ the concurrency and 2.3 to 3.7$\times$ the output, depending on the per-token bound. On DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash at 500K context and concurrency 512, Adaptive DHiSparse with DSpark delivers 1.85$\times$ the output of resident serving with DSpark. In a load step, optional residency reduces host fetches from 14.2% to 1.4% of selected rows at low load and releases memory under pressure.

</details>


### [157] [A Privileged Teacher That Only Scores Cannot Search: Critique-Conditioned On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13744)
*Mayug Maniparambil,Ayush Rai,Ekansh Verma,Noel E. O'Connor,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线策略自蒸馏中评分教师信号的有效性，发现其无法定位错误或提供修正性学分，提出批评条件自蒸馏方法，通过先生成批评再条件化评分教师，显著提升了错误定位和修正能力，并在数学推理任务和冷启动GRPO中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略自蒸馏试图用教师模型对学生生成轨迹的token级评分替代稀疏的可验证奖励信号，但作者质疑这种评分信号是否真正包含修正性学分。通过实验发现，全分布KL散度和token对数比率这两个自蒸馏目标使用的信号，既无法定位推理轨迹中的错误位置，也无法将概率从错误token推开，甚至无法在同一问题内区分正确与错误的轨迹。作者认为这是结构性缺陷：定位错误本身是一个搜索问题，而评分教师从未被显式要求执行该搜索。

Method: 提出批评条件自蒸馏：第一阶段，让模型生成对当前rollout的批评；第二阶段，将该批评作为上下文条件化评分教师，使其基于批评对rollout进行token级评分。利用这种批评条件化的评分信号进行蒸馏训练。同时在测试时，将批评加入上下文让模型从错误rollout恢复；在冷启动GRPO中，利用批评提供优势信号。

Result: 批评条件化评分信号能够：1）准确定位推理轨迹中的错误跨度；2）在错误跨度内具有修正性，将概率从错误token推开；3）将概率转移向触发回溯的token（如'Wait'、'But'、'Actually'）。测试时，带批评上下文恢复错误rollout能解决原本采样无法解决的数学问题。冷启动GRPO中，批评为可验证奖励无法提供信号的提示词提供了优势信号。

Conclusion: 传统自蒸馏的评分教师因缺乏显式搜索机制而无法提供有效修正信号。批评条件自蒸馏通过显式生成批评引导评分，解决了错误定位与修正的结构性缺陷，在推理任务和强化学习冷启动中均展现出显著优势，为密集奖励建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation promises to replace the sparse outcome signal of verifiable-reward RL with dense token-level credit: a teacher conditioned on the reference solution scores the student's own rollout, and the resulting distributional shift is distilled back into the policy. We investigate whether this scoring signal actually contains corrective credit. Using gold error spans in student rollouts, we test the two signals consumed by self-distillation objectives, the full-distribution KL and the token log-ratio, for two properties: whether they localize errors and whether they push probability away from erroneous student tokens. They exhibit neither of these properties nor can they reliably distinguish correct from incorrect rollouts within the same problem. We argue that this failure is structural: locating an error in a reasoning trace is itself a search problem, while a scoring teacher is never explicitly asked to perform that search. The same model, with the same privileged information, localizes errors substantially better when allowed to generate a critique. This forms the motivation for \emph{critique-conditioned self-distillation}: first generate a critique of the current rollout, then condition the scoring teacher on that critique. The resulting signal localizes errors, is corrective within error spans, and shifts probability toward tokens that initiate backtracking such as ``Wait'', ``But'', and ``Actually''. We further show that the critique is useful both at test time, where resuming a wrong rollout with the critique in context converts math problems the model cannot solve by sampling, and in cold-start GRPO, where it provides an advantage signal on prompts for which verifiable rewards alone provide none.

</details>


### [158] [UkrMeme: Multimodal Explainable Persuasion Detection in Ukrainian Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13783)
*Nazar Andrushko,Vera Schmitt,Veronika Solopova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究乌克兰战争梗图中的说服技巧检测，引入了UkrMeme数据集（200个有机产生的乌克兰战时梗图，标注说服技巧）和PTM-UA（Propaganda 950基准的乌克兰语翻译子集，保留原始视觉内容）。在零样本提示设置下评估视觉语言模型（VLMs）在图像、文本、多模态输入上的表现，使用层级F1指标。并对比了逐层相关性传播（LRP）和模型生成的token归因两种解释方法，分析各自强调的语义证据类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梗图在现代在线交流中扮演核心角色，特别是在政治冲突语境下，视觉和文本线索结合传达说服性信息。现有研究缺乏针对乌克兰语战时梗图的说服技巧检测数据集和评估基准，需要系统研究VLMs在此任务上的表现及可解释性。

Method: 1. 构建UkrMeme数据集：200个有机产生的乌克兰战时梗图，标注说服技巧。2. 创建PTM-UA：将Propaganda 950基准的子集翻译为乌克兰语，保留原始视觉内容。3. 零样本提示评估VLMs：在图像、文本、多模态三种输入模态下测试，采用层级F1指标评估。4. 解释性分析：对比LRP和模型生成的token归因方法，分析两者强调的语义证据类别差异。

Result: 论文在零样本设置下评估了多个VLMs在UkrMeme和PTM-UA上的说服技巧检测性能，层级F1指标揭示了多模态输入优于单模态。解释性分析显示LRP倾向于关注视觉区域的整体语义模式，而token归因更关注具体文本token的说服性语言特征，两种方法捕获的证据类别存在互补性。

Conclusion: 该工作为乌克兰语战时梗图的说服技巧检测提供了首个标注数据集和翻译基准，建立了VLMs零样本评估框架。研究揭示了多模态融合对检测性能的提升，以及不同解释方法在证据关注点上的差异，为政治传播语境下的多模态说服分析提供了方法论参考。

Abstract: Memes play a central role in modern online communication, especially in contexts of political conflict where visual and textual cues combine to convey persuasive messages. We study persuasive technique detection in Ukrainian memes using vision-language models (VLMs). We introduce UkrMeme, a dataset of 200 organically produced Ukrainian wartime memes annotated for persuasion techniques, and PTM-UA, a Ukrainian translation of a subset of the Propaganda 950 benchmark that preserves original visual content while translating meme text. We evaluate VLMs in a zero-shot prompting setup across image, text, and multimodal inputs using hierarchical F1 metrics. Finally, we compare Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) and model-generated token attributions and analyze the semantic categories of evidence emphasized by each explanation method.

</details>


### [159] [Studying Comics at Scale with Artificial Intelligence: The PAGE Framework and Three Studies of American Comics and Japanese Manga](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13840)
*Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAGE框架，利用人工智能（包括生成模型）大规模分析漫画档案，实现面板解析、文本提取、书目记录、视觉语言模型标注、人工标注校验及跨档案变化解释，并在美国漫画（1938-1956）和日本漫画两大语料库上验证了三项研究能力：政治权力表征恢复、视觉形式测量与艺术家识别、跨文化语言角色追踪。同时指出该工具可用于档案可搜索化、无障碍阅读、藏品保护与艺术家署名等应用，勾勒AI强化漫画研究、保护与传播的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漫画作为20世纪重要视觉记录，海量页面（数十万页）却从未作为整体被系统研究，现有漫画研究多停留在单本书层面。作者主张人工智能（特别是生成模型）可改变这一现状，使大规模、计算化的漫画学术研究成为可能，从而造福漫画媒介本身。

Method: 提出PAGE框架，包含四大核心步骤：1）将每张扫描页解析为面板、文本及书目记录；2）依据书面编码手册，用视觉语言模型对每页进行标注，模拟人工编码员流程；3）抽取样本进行人工标注以校准模型阅读；4）在控制出版商和体裁的条件下，解释档案跨越时空的变化。实证部分针对3,929册美国漫画（1938-1956）和87卷日本漫画，设计三项研究分别验证文本检索+视觉阅读、视觉形式测量+艺术家识别、跨传统发言角色追踪三种能力。

Result: 三项研究分别实现：1）结合文本检索与视觉阅读，复原大众媒介对政治权力的表征；2）测量整个行业的页面视觉形式，识别出个别艺术家的创作风格；3）追踪两大漫画传统中“谁在说话”，并经人工标注核验。此外，同一工具集还能使档案按绘图内容可搜、支持盲/弱视读者阅读、辅助脆弱藏品保护及为无署名艺术家正名。

Conclusion: 人工智能可成为漫画研究的工具性基础设施，PAGE框架展示了从大规模解析、标注、校验到跨档案解释的完整流程，并在两大漫画传统上验证了多种研究能力。作者主张未来研究议程应聚焦于利用AI加强漫画的研究深度、保护力度与传播广度，推动漫画学从单本研究转向计算规模的整体研究。

Abstract: Comics are one of the great visual records of the twentieth century: hundreds of thousands of pages in which a mass readership saw its leaders, its wars and its heroes. Most of this record has never been studied as a whole; comics scholarship proceeds one book at a time. We argue that artificial intelligence, including generative models, can change this to the benefit of the medium itself. We propose PAGE, a framework that makes artificial intelligence an instrument for comics research. It parses every scan into panels, text and a bibliographic record; annotates each page with a vision-language model that follows a written codebook, as a human coder would; grounds samples of these readings in human annotation; and explains change across an archive, with estimates controlled for publisher and genre. We demonstrate the framework on 3,929 American comic books (1938--1956) and 87 volumes of Japanese manga, in three studies, each resting on a different capability. The first pairs text search with visual reading to recover how a mass medium portrayed political power. The second measures the page's visual form across an industry and recovers the hand of individual artists. The third follows who speaks across two comics traditions, checked against human annotation. Beyond research, the same instruments can make archives searchable by what is drawn, open comics to blind and low-vision readers, and help preserve fragile collections and credit uncredited artists. We outline a research agenda in which artificial intelligence strengthens the study, preservation and reach of the medium.

</details>


### [160] [X-AgentRed: Red-Teaming Personal Agents in Evolving Multi-Service Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13886)
*Yeonsung Jung,Zhiyuan Wang,Zifan Wang,Vítor Albiero,Benjamin Newman,Edoardo Debenedetti,Ivan Evtimov,Ninareh Mehrabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 X-AgentRed，一种自进化红队测试框架，通过工具依赖图和执行反馈系统性发现跨服务工具调用路径中的组合性危害，显著提高有害路径发现率和工具覆盖度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人代理通过工具连接多种网络服务，其动作累积交互可能导致意外有害结果。现有红队方法依赖 LLM 生成或人工分解有害目标，受限于训练数据分布，倾向于熟悉的工具组合，难以系统探索新颖的跨服务交互路径。

Method: X-AgentRed 构建有向工具依赖图，节点为工具，边表示前工具的效果满足后工具的执行前置条件。通过分析各工具的效果、潜在危害和前置条件，从图中采样“配方”实例化为真实场景。利用执行反馈进行自我改进：对成功路径进行工具替换以多样化，对失败路径在阻塞步骤细化前置条件以继续执行。

Result: 在四个前沿代理模型上，X-AgentRed 的有害路径发现率是基线 STAC 的 1.77 倍，平均工具覆盖度达到 STAC 的 2.99 倍，探索的工具集合更加多样。

Conclusion: 基于工具依赖图和执行反馈的自进化红队方法能有效突破 LLM 生成偏见，系统性挖掘跨服务组合性危害，为代理安全评估提供更全面、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Personal agents increasingly connect to services across the web and applications by using tools. As the effects of their actions accumulate and interact across services, unintended or harmful outcomes can arise when agents fulfill benign user requests. Proactively identifying these compositional failures requires systematically exploring diverse cross-service paths. Existing red-teaming methods often simply prompt LLMs to identify potentially harmful paths from task and environment descriptions or decompose harmful objectives specified by humans or LLMs into seemingly benign action combinations. However, generation is biased toward patterns learned during training, potentially favoring familiar tool combinations and leaving novel cross-service interactions underexplored. To address this, we propose X-AgentRed, a self-evolving red-teaming framework that discovers harmful tool call paths through a graph of tool dependencies and expands and refines them using execution feedback. By analyzing each tool's effects, potential harms, and preconditions for execution, the red-teaming agent constructs a directed graph that captures how one tool's effects can satisfy another's execution preconditions. Recipes sampled from this graph are instantiated as realistic scenarios in which agents can produce harmful outcomes. Execution feedback enables self-improvement by diversifying paths that produce harm through tool substitution and refining preconditions at blocked steps in unsuccessful paths to enable further execution. Across four frontier agentic models, X-AgentRed achieves $1.77\times$ the harmful path rate of STAC and explores a more diverse set of tools, yielding $2.99\times$ the tool coverage on average.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Algebraic Integer Quantization for Machine Learning Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12494)
*Anthony Kostal-Vazquez,Vassil Dimitrov,Branden Butler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出代数整数数字格式（AINF）作为高度可配置且精确的压缩计算框架，并引入代数整数量化（AIQ）用于大语言模型推理，在等效比特预算下达到与工业标准MX格式具有竞争力的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浮点和定点格式在压缩计算中存在精度损失和配置灵活性不足的问题。代数整数具有可还原为纯整数运算的特性，能实现无误差的买累加操作，且可根据数值特性选择适合的代数整数，提供浮点/定点格式无法获得的优化机会。

Method: 1) 提出AINF框架，利用代数整数可还原为整数运算的特性开发矩阵乘法引擎；2) 引入AIQ作为LLM推理的有损压缩格式，使用二次整数（度为2的代数整数）进行实验；3) 在多种模型和基准上与MX格式对比验证。

Result: AINF矩阵乘法引擎性能在现代硬件加速器上达到最高效数值对应版本的可预测因子范围内。AIQ在等效比特预算下，跨多样化模型和基准测试达到与工业标准MX格式具有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: AINF/AIQ为压缩计算提供了新范式，二次整数已验证有效性，高阶和多维代数整数有望带来更大计算优势，是未来值得探索的方向。

Abstract: In this article, we propose algebraic integer number formats (AINF) as a framework for highly configurable and accurate compressed computation. A key property of AINF is its straightforward reduction to purely integer arithmetic, enabling error-free multiply-accumulate operations. We develop a matrix multiplication engine that exploits this property, obtaining performance within a predictable factor of the most efficient numerical counterparts on modern hardware accelerators. Another key advantage of AIQ is the freedom to select algebraic integers tailored to the numerical characteristics of a given use case, offering optimization opportunities that are fundamentally unavailable in floating-point or fixed-point formats. Motivated by these properties, we introduce algebraic integer quantization (AIQ), a lossy compression format for large language model (LLM) inference, and provide results demonstrating that AIQ achieves competitive accuracy compared to the industry-standard MX format across a diverse set of models and benchmarks at equivalent bit-per-parameter budgets. The experiments presented in this article employ algebraic integers of degree two (quadratic integers); generalizations to higher-order and, in particular, multidimensional algebraic integers promise even greater computational advantages and remain a promising direction for future work.

</details>


### [162] [Kairos: Toward a Byte-Level Foundation Model for Every Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12495)
*Fabien Furfaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Kairos 是一个面向单机训练的小型多模态语言模型，融合了双向门控 DeltaNet 线性注意力与滑动窗口注意力（LiZAttention2）、块级注意力残差聚合、稀疏 MoE 前馈层及多尺度字节级编解码器，采用均匀噪声去噪目标在统一字节空间上训练。论文通过在单一 440 字节句子上过拟合实验（1–15M 参数，仅文本）验证实现能否收敛：两种注意力变体在 3000 步后损失降至 ~10⁻³；600 步后不同编解码器的平均最小损失从 0.018（patch 编解码）到 0.354（深度可分离卷积编解码），三种随机种子排名一致。共享投影对比独立投影的优势随步数增加或数据多样化而消失，冻结投影时甚至反转，属收敛速度效应而非容量效应。实验仅证明实现可优化，未涉及泛化、多模态训练或与现有模型对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单机资源受限下训练小型语言模型，架构试错空间极小。作者希望先验证组合架构的实现是否能正确优化（收敛），再考虑其在多模态任务上的实用性。

Method: 1. 架构组件：LiZAttention2（双向门控 DeltaNet 线性注意力 + 共享投影的滑动窗口注意力）、块级 Attention-Residual 聚合、稀疏 MoE FFN、多尺度字节级编解码器。2. 训练目标：统一字节空间上的均匀噪声去噪。3. 收敛理智检查：在单一 440 字节句子上过拟合，参数量 1–15M，仅文本模态；三随机种子测试编解码器与注意力选择，单次运行扫描七个额外因子。

Result: • 两种注意力变体（LiZAttention2 与纯窗口注意力）在 3000 步均达到 ~10⁻³ 最小损失。• 600 步时，不同编解码器的平均最小损失为 0.018（patch）至 0.354（深度可分离卷积），三种种子排名完全一致。• 共享注意力投影相对独立投影的优势随训练步数增加或数据多样化而消失，冻结投影时优势反转，表明是收敛速度效应而非模型容量效应。

Conclusion: 实验表明 Kairos 的实现能正确优化（收敛），但未评估泛化能力、多模态联合训练效果或与现有模型的对比。后续计划通过大规模预训练解决这些问题。讨论中给出了 200M 总参数/25M 激活参数的理论规模估算，吞吐率数据均为理论推算而非实测。代码已开源。

Abstract: Small language models trained on a single local machine leave little room for architectural trial and error. Kairos combines bidirectional gated-DeltaNet linear attention and sliding-window attention over shared projections (LiZAttention2), block-wise Attention-Residual aggregation, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) feed-forward layer and a multi-scale byte-level codec, trained with a uniform-noise denoising objective over one byte space shared by text, image, audio, video, lidar and control streams; this report asks whether the combination optimizes at all before asking whether it is useful. We give code-derived algorithms for each component, including a cross-session recurrent-state memory gate, then run convergence sanity checks: overfittinga single 440-byte sentence at 1--15M parameters, text only, with three seeds for codec and attention choices and single-run sweeps for seven further factors. The two attention variants tested at 3\,000 steps (LiZAttention2 and window attention alone) reach a minimum loss near $10^{-3}$. After 600 steps, the mean minimum loss ranges from 0.018 (patch codec) to 0.354 (depthwise convolutional codec) with an identical ranking on three seeds. An apparent advantage of shared over separate attention projections closes with more steps or more diverse data and reverses when the projections are frozen: it is a convergence-speed effect, not a capacity effect. These checks suggest that the implementation optimizes; they say nothing about generalization, multimodal training or comparison with existing models, which a planned pretraining run is meant to address. The Discussion sizes a 200M-total/25M-active target analytically; none of its throughput figures is measured. Code is available at https://github.com/fabienfrfr/Kairos . Python package at https://pypi.org/project/kairos-fm/ .

</details>


### [163] [An Input-Dependent Readout Phase for Resonant Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12496)
*Shivesh Rane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种改进ResonatorLM的方法，将读出相位设为当前token的函数，仅在读取环节引入输入依赖，保持卷积精确且仅增加0.2%参数。实验表明该方法在训练长度外泛化显著优于原模型，困惑度从12.81降至3.76。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定状态大小的序列模型避免了注意力机制的二次方复杂度，但面临设计权衡：输入依赖的状态转移虽提供内容相关记忆，却需FFT训练；固定转移虽保留O(T log T)卷积路径，但对所有输入以相同方式读取记忆。ResonatorLM属于后者，通过固定学习相位读取阻尼复振子库。作者旨在仅在读取环节引入输入依赖，保持训练效率同时增强泛化能力。

Method: 将ResonatorLM的读出相位改为当前token的函数，仅在读取时引入输入依赖，保持状态转移固定，从而维持精确卷积且仅增加0.2%参数。分析未训练振子库发现相位编码逝去时间，但普通token在2-8步内低于背景，无阻尼选择能将线性重构延伸至16 token以上。在6M参数字符级模型上实验，训练长度256，测试长度1024。

Result: 输入依赖相位在训练长度内与原模型持平，但在外推至1024 token时困惑度从12.81大幅降至3.76。失效归因于长记忆头累积的历史超出训练曝光范围。学习到的相位追踪当前字符而非距离，并未改善检索。

Conclusion: 仅在读取环节引入输入依赖的相位函数，能以极小参数开销显著提升固定状态模型的长程泛化能力，揭示了原模型失效源于长记忆头过度累积未见历史，且学习相位实质编码token身份而非时间距离。

Abstract: Sequence models with a fixed-size state avoid the quadratic cost of attention, but face a design choice. An input-dependent state transition gives content-dependent memory at the cost of FFT-based training, while a fixed transition keeps the O(T log T) convolutional path but reads memory the same way for every input. ResonatorLM, a recent model of the second kind, reads a bank of damped complex oscillators through a fixed learned phase per head. We make that readout phase a function of the current token, which adds input dependence to the read only, keeps the convolution exact, and costs 0.2 percent more parameters. An analysis of the untrained bank shows that phase does encode elapsed time, but an ordinary token falls below the background within 2-8 steps and no choice of damping extends linear reconstruction beyond about 16 tokens. In 6M-parameter character-level models on WikiText-2, the input-dependent phase matches the original at the training length and removes its failure beyond it: trained on 256 tokens and tested on 1024, perplexity falls from 12.81 to 3.76. We trace the failure to long-memory heads that accumulate more history than training exposed them to. The learned phase tracks the current character rather than distance and does not improve retrieval.

</details>


### [164] [CircuitTree: Composing Circuits into A Syllable Transcriber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12520)
*Jiankun Wei,Ewan Dunbar,Gerald Penn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贪心密度缩放的电路组合算法及 CircuitTree 数据结构，实现了从 HuBERT 中剪枝得到的 39 个音素电路到音节分类器的组合，构建层级电路流水线在开音节转写任务上达到 64.8% 准确率，仅比无共享电路的上界低 1.2 个百分点，且 99.5% 电路组件被共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路发现旨在将整体神经网络分解为可解释的模块化子网络，但现有方法难以将多个电路高效组合为单一模型，且简单并集存在局限性。

Method: 提出贪心密度缩放组合算法，克服简单并集的局限；推广到 N 个电路，设计 CircuitTree 数据结构，基于 Θ(N log N) 树状组合调度；将从 HuBERT 剪枝得到的音素电路组合为音节分类器，再串联成层级电路流水线。

Result: 在开音节转写任务上达到 64.8% 准确率，距离无共享电路的 66.0% 上界仅差 1.2 个百分点，同时实现 99.5% 的电路组件共享。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了可通过高效组合大量小型可解释电路构建复杂功能模型，在保持高性能的同时实现极高的参数共享，为模块化、可解释的神经网络工程提供了实用路径。

Abstract: The goal of circuit discovery is to decompose a monolithic neural network into tiny modular subnetworks which are interpretable in the sense that each does an identifiable subtask. We develop a practical method for composing arbitrarily many circuits into a single model. We propose a greedy, density-scaled composition algorithm that overcomes limitations of previous attempts to compose circuits by naive union, then generalize it to $N$ circuits via CircuitTree, a new data structure built on a $Θ(N\log N)$ tree-compose schedule. We compose circuits pruned from HuBERT for classifying individual phonemes into a syllable classifier, a capability none of the 39 phoneme circuits has individually. Chaining CircuitTrees into a hierarchy-of-circuits pipeline, we transcribe open syllables at 64.8\% accuracy, within 1.2 points of a 66.0\% ceiling that shares no circuitry at all, with 99.5\% of circuit components shared.

</details>


### [165] [Behind-the-Meter Photovoltaic Visibility from Net Smart Meters: Deployment Regimes and Operational Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12527)
*Jack Zheng,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度学习光伏功率解聚在不同部署场景下的泛化性能，提出四种部署机制区分户际迁移与时域外推，基于Ausgrid数据并在Pecan Street数据上验证跨域适应。实验表明部署机制对误差影响远大于模型选择，微调仅需5户目标域数据即可显著降低迁移误差，长上下文窗口利于已知户外推但对新户效果因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网运营商需要掌握用户侧光伏发电功率，但智能电表仅测得净负荷。深度学习解聚虽可恢复光伏功率，其实际运营价值取决于模型在已知用户、新用户、未来时段、新区域四种部署场景下的泛化能力。现有研究缺乏系统量化不同部署机制对解聚性能的影响。

Method: 构建四种部署机制（已知户/新户 × 同期/未来期）分离户际迁移与时域外推；采用固定训练协议基准测试代表性深度序列架构；在Ausgrid智能电表数据上训练，通过目标域归一化与少样本微调（5户）实现向Pecan Street数据集的跨域适应；固定48小时上下文长度评估日间MAE。

Result: 最优模型在已知户未来期日间MAE为0.104 kW，新户同期上升至0.185 kW；部署机制解释的误差方差远超模型选择；目标域归一化下微调5户可降低迁移误差28%-36%；长输入窗口改善已知户外推，对新户效果因模型异。

Conclusion: 建议制定匹配部署机制的误差预算，新区域部署时利用少量目标监督数据微调适配，根据具体应用场景选择上下文窗口长度，以提升能源数据分析的实用性。

Abstract: Distribution operators need visibility of behind-the-meter photovoltaic (PV) generation, yet most residential customers are observed only through net smart meters. Deep-learning-based PV disaggregation can restore this visibility, but its operational value depends on whether the model serves known customers, new customers, future periods, or new regions. We quantify this dependence using four deployment regimes that separate household transfer from temporal extrapolation, applied to Ausgrid smart-meter data with cross-dataset adaptation to the Pecan Street data. Using a fixed training protocol, we benchmark representative deep sequence architectures for PV disaggregation under regime-matched evaluation. At a fixed 48-hour context length, daylight mean absolute error (MAE) of the best model rises from 0.104~kW on seen households in the future test period to 0.185~kW on unseen households over the same period, and under a fixed training contract the deployment regime explains far more of the error variation than model choice. Under target-domain normalization, fine-tuning on five target-region households reduces source-model transfer error by 28\%--36\%. Long input windows help known-customer extrapolation, while their new-customer benefits vary by model. These findings suggest regime-matched error budgets, limited target supervision for model adaptation in new regions, and use-case-dependent context selection for energy data analytics.

</details>


### [166] [GlucoPRISM: Protocol-Supervised Pretraining for Cross-Cohort Glucose Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12528)
*Adinath Madhavrao Dukre,Abdullah Mohammad,Yifan Lu,Eran Segal,Ahmed Metwally,Imran Razzak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlucoPRISM 通过响应契约将 CGM 基础模型的表示解耦为 Trait、State、Sensor 三个频段，冻结编码器后丢弃 Sensor 频段即可在无目标数据的情况下显著提升跨队列迁移性能（平均 AUROC 从 68.75 提升至 73.57），且保留频段在 14 个公开任务-队列单元中 13 个提升窗口 AUROC（p=0.031）及 15 个临床终点中 14 个提升相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CGM 基础模型将个体特征、日内状态变化与传感器测量差异混合在同一向量中，导致设备相关的测量偏差随表示进入新队列，严重损害跨队列迁移性能。需要一种无需目标标签、无需微调即可剥离有害测量成分的方法。

Method: 提出 GlucoPRISM：在 GlucoFM 主干上施加响应契约，将 128 维表示强制解耦为三个频段——64 维 Trait 频段（对日期与传感器均不变）、48 维 State 频段（仅随日期变化）、16 维 Sensor 频段（仅追踪设备）。利用合成传感器视角（基于配对 Dexcom/Libre 录制校准）、不同日期配对及葡萄糖摘要目标提供自监督信号，无需临床标签。部署时冻结编码器，直接投影丢弃 Sensor 频段。

Result: 在十二个跨队列方向上，丢弃 Sensor 频段后平均迁移 AUROC 从 68.75 提升至 73.57（GlucoFM 为 69.3）。增益来自两部分：在双传感器队列 CGMacros 中过滤带来 11-19 点提升（半样本重新校准后依然成立）；其余八个方向过滤中性，但编码器本身已超越 GlucoFM 3.5 点。设备在过滤后仍可线性解码，证明仅移除了有害迁移的设备相关成分而非所有测量信息。保留频段在 14 个公开任务-队列单元中 13 个提升窗口 AUROC（Holm 校正 p=0.031），在 9,303 人外部队列的 15 个临床终点中 14 个提升相关性。

Conclusion: GlucoPRISM 通过结构化响应契约将测量偏差定位至可显式丢弃的 Sensor 频段，实现了零目标数据、零微调的跨队列迁移增强，且保留的表示在多样下游任务与临床终点上稳健提升性能，为可部署的医疗基础模型提供了可解释、可控的解耦范式。

Abstract: Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) foundation models encode each day as one vector that mixes who the person is, what happened that day and how the sensor measured it, so measurement differences travel with the representation into new cohorts. We ask whether pretraining can place the measurement component that harms transfer in known coordinates, so that deployment drops them with a fixed projection and no target data. GlucoPRISM supervises a GlucoFM backbone with response contracts, rules for how each part of the representation should change when only the day or only the sensor changes: a 64-dimensional Trait band should change with neither, a 48-dimensional State band only across days, and a 16-dimensional Sensor band should track the device. Synthetic sensor views calibrated on paired Dexcom and Libre recordings, different-day pairs and glucose-summary targets supply this supervision without clinical labels. Across twelve cross-cohort directions, dropping the Sensor band from the same frozen encoder raises mean transfer AUROC from 68.75 to 73.57, against 69.3 for GlucoFM. The gain has two parts. Into CGMacros, the one target cohort recorded with two sensor types, filtering adds 11 to 19 points, and this holds when the generator is recalibrated on half of the participants and scored on the other half. On the other eight directions filtering is neutral and the encoder itself exceeds GlucoFM by 3.5 points. The device remains linearly decodable after filtering, so the filter removes a device-related component that harms transfer rather than all measurement information. The retained bands improve window AUROC on thirteen of fourteen public task--cohort cells (Holm-adjusted p=0.031) and correlation on fourteen of fifteen clinical endpoints in an external cohort of 9,303 participants.

</details>


### [167] [CacheScope: Exact-Compute Allocation for Training-Free Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12530)
*Qinhan Lyu,Jusheng Zhang,Zhixing Zhang,Hao Liu,Zijie Liang,Jin Jiang,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无需训练的特征复用加速方法 CacheScope，通过在线识别高风险复用步骤并选择性回退到全去噪计算，在多个扩散Transformer模型上实现高达3.57倍加速并显著降低LPIPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征复用方法主要改进跳过特征的重构，但研究发现一旦使用稳定的一阶重构器，增加重构复杂度收益有限，而精确计算步骤的位置对保真度影响更大。且绝对浅层探针变化受输入轨迹位移和网络响应强度共同影响，不能直接反映缓存风险。

Method: 提出归一化探针输出变化与对应输入位移，得到单位轨迹移动的方向性增益信号。基于此设计 CacheScope，在线识别高风险复用步骤并选择性回退到全去噪计算，保持底层缓存重构器不变。无需额外训练、梯度、辅助网络或提示词特定的离线调度优化。

Result: 在 FLUX.1-dev、Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 和 Open-Sora 1.2 上实现高达 3.57 倍加速，相比最强探针基线将中位数 LPIPS 降低高达 70.1%。

Conclusion: CacheScope 通过归一化探针变化解决缓存风险评估的混淆因素，实现高效无训练的扩散Transformer推理加速，显著提升生成质量与速度的平衡。

Abstract: Training-free feature reuse accelerates diffusion transformer inference by replacing full denoising computation at selected steps with cached approximations. Existing methods mainly improve the reconstruction of skipped features, but we find that once a stable first-order reconstructor is used, increasing reconstruction complexity brings limited gains, whereas the placement of exact-computation steps has a much larger effect on fidelity. More importantly, absolute shallow-probe variation is jointly affected by input-trajectory displacement and network response strength, and therefore does not directly reflect cache risk. We address this confounding by normalizing probe-output variation with the corresponding input displacement, yielding a directional gain per unit trajectory movement. Based on this signal, we propose CacheScope, which identifies high-risk reuse steps online and selectively falls back to full denoising computation while leaving the underlying cache reconstructor unchanged. CacheScope requires no additional training, gradients, auxiliary networks, or prompt-specific offline schedule optimization. Across FLUX.1-dev, Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B, and Open-Sora 1.2, CacheScope achieves up to 3.57x acceleration and reduces median LPIPS by up to 70.1% over the strongest probe-based baseline.

</details>


### [168] [Beyond Temporal Proximity: Explanatory World Models for Credit Assignment in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13504)
*Oriol Corcoll,Qiyao Wei,Ciarán Mark Gilligan-Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出解释性世界模型(EWM)，利用大语言模型生成的解释作为学习信号，通过保守层级算子重新分配延迟奖励，解决生成式推荐中长时程强化学习的信用分配问题，在多个基准和工业级系统上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐系统越来越多地采用强化学习优化长期结果，但标准RL按时间邻近性而非显式相关性传播奖励，导致对遥远但重要动作的信号衰减，造成样本效率低下。需要一种能识别早期事件对最终结果因果贡献的机制。

Method: 提出解释性世界模型(EWM)框架：使用前沿大语言模型生成解释，识别对结果有贡献的早期事件；设计保守层级算子递归重新分配奖励，同时保持无折扣回报不变；实例化三种互补解释变体，分别基于Halpern的实际因果、Lombrozo的解释简洁性和Deutsch的难变性解释理论。

Result: 在Crafter和WordCraft两个长时程RL基准上，EWM大幅超越PPO和LaRe，奖励延迟越大优势越明显；在KuaiRand序列推荐基准上，离线超越SFT和LaRe，在线超越A2C；在工业级生成式推荐器离线评估中，显著提升Recall和行为代理指标。

Conclusion: 解释可以为从延迟结果中学习提供结构化且可复用的信用分配，解释性世界模型通过利用大语言模型的解释能力，有效解决了长时程强化学习中的时间信用分配难题，为生成式推荐系统的长期优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Generative Recommenders increasingly use Reinforcement Learning (RL) to optimize outcomes that may appear hundreds or thousands of interactions after a decision. Standard RL propagates reward according to temporal proximity rather than explicit relevance, attenuating the signal assigned to distant but important actions and creating a source of sample inefficiency. We introduce Explanatory World Models (EWM), an attribution framework that uses explanations to identify the earlier events that plausibly contributed to a realized outcome. We instantiate EWM with a frontier language model and use its explanations as learning signals using a conservative hierarchical operator that recursively redistributes reward while preserving the undiscounted episode return. To investigate what constitutes a useful explanation, we operationalize three complementary variants inspired by Halpern's actual causation, Lombrozo's explanatory simplicity, and Deutsch's hard-to-vary explanations. We evaluate EWM on two controlled long-horizon RL benchmarks, a public sequential-recommendation benchmark, and an industry-scale generative recommender. On Crafter and WordCraft, EWM substantially outperforms PPO and LaRe, with its largest gains when rewards are most delayed. On KuaiRand, EWM outperforms SFT and LaRe offline and A2C online. Finally, in an offline evaluation of an industry generative recommender, EWM considerably improves Recall and increases behavioral proxies. These results suggest that explanations can provide structured and reusable credit for learning from delayed outcomes.

</details>


### [169] [Market Impact Estimation via Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12537)
*Dennis Thumm,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种因果市场模拟器，利用连续时间因果先验数据拟合网络（PFN）解决大单市场冲击估计中的混淆偏差问题。在合成先验上，朴素估计器偏差高达68%，而PFN能追踪无偏真值。零样本迁移至ABIDES限价订单簿、加密货币永续合约和纳斯达克股票数据均表现良好，循环状态空间变体进一步将冲击形状相关性从0.68提升至0.92-0.96。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统市场冲击模型（如Almgren-Chriss、传播子）通过回归价格于订单流来估计冲击，但当知情订单流同时通过信息和交易双重渠道移动价格时，这种回归会产生混淆偏差。核心问题是因果性的：大单执行时应问'若我不交易价格会是多少？'而非'价格会是多少？'。现有方法未显式处理隐性信息流对流价关系的混淆。

Method: 构建合成市场先验，其中隐性信息流按可控强度混淆流价关系；训练连续时间因果先验数据拟合网络（PFN）学习因果干预响应；引入'do-消融孪生模型'移除干预输入作为基准；零样本迁移至ABIDES模拟器、加密货币永续合约（BTC/ETH/SOL）和纳斯达克股票实盘数据；提出循环状态空间变体改进冲击随时间累积的形状；使用分裂共形重校准修复区间校准。

Result: 合成先验上：朴素估计器偏差符合闭式解（高达68%高估），PFN在高混淆下追踪无偏真值神谕，do-消融模型崩塌至预窗口均值。零样本迁移：ABIDES中对元订单响应达+0.38前置标准差（种子重放反事实基准）；在2300个加密货币流爆发和3800个纳斯达克股票流爆发上复现干预响应。离先验性能优雅降级，分裂共形重校准修复区间校准。迁移关键在于将元订单编码为附加流而非钳制价位。循环状态空间变体将冲击形状相关性从0.68提升至0.92-0.96，但因训练配方导致先验上去混淆能力稍弱。

Conclusion: 因果PFN能有效去混淆估计市场冲击，在合成先验上达无偏，并展现出跨多种市场的零样本因果迁移证据。将元订单建模为流增量而非价格钳制是迁移关键。循环状态空间变体显著改进冲击随时间的累积形状，但揭示训练配方而非循环结构本身导致了先验上去混淆的权衡。分裂共形重校准提供可靠的不确定性量化。

Abstract: When a trader executes a large order, the relevant question is not \emph{what will the price be?} but \emph{what would the price have been had I not traded?} Market impact is thus a causal quantity, yet classical models (Almgren--Chriss, propagators) regress price on flow, which is biased whenever informed flow moves prices through information as well as trading. We present a proof-of-concept \emph{Causal Market Simulator}: a continuous-time causal prior-data fitted network (PFN) trained on a synthetic market prior in which a hidden informational flow confounds the flow--price relation by construction, with confounding strength an exact per-episode knob. On this prior, the naive estimator's bias follows a closed form we confirm empirically (up to 68\% overestimation), while the PFN tracks the unbiased true-parameter oracle at high confounding. Its \emph{do-ablated twin}, the same model with the intervention inputs removed, collapses onto the pre-window mean. Zero-shot, the model responds at the ground-truth scale to metaorders in an ABIDES limit-order book ($+0.38$ pre-onset standard deviations against seed-replay counterfactuals), and its intervention response replicates on about 2,300 detected flow bursts in BTC/ETH/SOL perpetuals and 3,800 on Nasdaq equities, evidence consistent with, though not proof of, causal transfer. Off-prior the response degrades gracefully, and split-conformal recalibration repairs interval calibration. Transfer hinges on encoding the metaorder as added flow rather than a clamped price level, not on more training. A recurrent state-space variant closes the remaining shape gap: its impact accrues over execution duration where the transformer's stays flat, raising the correlation with measured metaorder impact from $+0.68$ to between $+0.92$ and $+0.96$, at the cost of weaker on-prior deconfounding that traces to the training recipe rather than to recurrence.

</details>


### [170] [Efficient Learning of Survival Analysis Models Under Outcome-Dependent Truncation and Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12538)
*Itai Zilberstein,Ioannis Anagnostides,Arman Kilic,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将截断学习与生存分析相结合，证明在基线风险已知时，Cox回归可在截断和右删失数据下通过一阶优化高效学习；但联合学习基线风险时，负对数似然包含严格次优局部极小值。实验表明考虑截断能减少预测偏差，扩展至神经网络，且比EM算法快数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习模型通常假设训练数据代表未来预测任务，但实际数据集常违反此假设。本文聚焦结果依赖截断（观测值是否出现在训练数据中取决于其标签），现有截断生存分析方法依赖EM等迭代过程，未刻画优化景观。

Method: 理论分析证明：在基线风险已知时，截断和右删失数据下的Cox回归可通过一阶优化高效学习（尽管非凸）；当基线风险联合学习时，构造可识别且正确指定的模型，证明负对数似然存在严格次优局部极小值。

Result: 在四个生存数据集实验中，考虑截断可减少朴素Cox回归的预测偏差，方法可扩展至神经网络，且相比标准EM算法实现数量级加速。

Conclusion: 本文弥合了截断高效学习与生存分析的文献鸿沟，为已知基线风险下的Cox回归一阶优化提供理论保证，同时揭示联合学习基线风险时的根本优化挑战（次优局部极小值）。
